$$
\ddot {x} _ {2} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x _ {2} - 2 x _ {0} x _ {1} = 0\tag{5}
$$

$x_{0}$ 需满足初始条件: t=0 时, $x_{0}=A$ , $\dot{x}_{0}=0$ .

$x_{1}$ 需满足初始条件: $t = 0$ 时, $x_{1} = 0$ , $\dot{x}_{1} = 0$ .

$x_{2}$ 需满足初始条件: t=0 时, $x_{2}=0$ , $\dot{x}_{2}=0$ .

立即可写出满足初始条件的式(3)的解为

$$
x _ {0} = A \cos \omega_ {0} t\tag{6}
$$

将式(6)代入式(4)

$$
\ddot {x} _ {1} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x _ {1} = A ^ {2} \cos^ {2} \omega_ {0} t = \frac {1}{2} A ^ {2} (1 + \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t)\tag{7}
$$

找此非齐次方程的特解.

设

$$
x _ {1} ^ {*} = \frac {A ^ {2}}{2 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} + B \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t
$$

代入式(7)，可定出

$$
B = - \frac {1}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} A ^ {2}
$$

故式(7)的通解可写成

$$
x _ {1} = c _ {1 1} \cos \omega_ {0} t + c _ {1 2} \sin \omega_ {0} t + \frac {A ^ {2}}{2 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} - \frac {A ^ {2}}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t
$$

初始条件: t=0 时, $x_{1}=0$ , $\dot{x}_{1}=0$ , 定 $c_{11}$ 、 $c_{12}$ , 得

$$
x _ {1} = - \frac {A ^ {2}}{3 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos \omega_ {0} t + \frac {A ^ {2}}{2 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} - \frac {A ^ {2}}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t\tag{8}
$$

一级近似解为

$$
x = x _ {0} + \lambda x _ {1} = A \cos \omega_ {0} t + \frac {\lambda A ^ {2}}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} (3 - 2 \cos \omega_ {0} t - \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t)
$$

下面求二级近似解．将式(6)、(8)代入式(5)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {x} _ {2} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x _ {2} & = 2 A \cos \omega_ {0} t \left[ \frac {A}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} (3 - 2 \cos \omega_ {0} t - \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t) \right] \\ & = \frac {A ^ {3}}{\omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos \omega_ {0} t - \frac {2 A ^ {3}}{3 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos^ {2} \omega_ {0} t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos \omega_ {0} t \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t \\ & = \frac {A ^ {3}}{\omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos \omega_ {0} t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} (1 + \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t) - \frac {A ^ {3}}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} (\cos 3 \omega_ {0} t + \cos \omega_ {0} t) \\ & = - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} + \frac {5 A ^ {3}}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos \omega_ {0} t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{6 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos 3 \omega_ {0} t \end{array}
$$

其中非齐次项 $\frac{5A^3}{6\omega_0^2}\cos \omega_0t$ 是引发共振的力项，而 $x_{2}$ 应是有限的， $\lambda^2 x_2$ 为二级小量，可见此题求二级近似解不能用通常的微扰法。

下面改用处理长期项的办法*求满足初始条件 $t = 0$ 时， $x = A, \dot{x} = 0$ 的一级和二级近似解。

将 $\omega_0^2$ 也展成 $\lambda$ 的幂级数.

$$
\omega_ {0} ^ {2} = \omega^ {2} + \alpha_ {1} \lambda + \alpha_ {2} \lambda^ {2}\tag{9}
$$

将式(2)、(9)代入式(1)，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\ddot {x} _ {0} + \lambda \ddot {x} _ {1} + \lambda^ {2} \ddot {x} _ {2} + (\omega^ {2} + \alpha_ {1} \lambda + \alpha_ {2} \lambda^ {2}) (x _ {0} + \lambda x _ {1} + \lambda^ {2} x _ {2})} \\ & {- \lambda (x _ {0} + \lambda x _ {1} + \lambda^ {2} x _ {2}) ^ {2} = 0} \\ & {\ddot {x} _ {0} + \omega^ {2} x _ {0} + \lambda (\ddot {x} _ {1} + \omega^ {2} x _ {1} + \alpha_ {1} x _ {0} - x _ {0} ^ {2})} \\ & {+ \lambda^ {2} (\ddot {x} _ {2} + \omega^ {2} x _ {2} + \alpha_ {1} x _ {1} + \alpha_ {2} x _ {0} - 2 x _ {0} x _ {1}) + \dots = 0} \end{array}
$$

$\lambda$ 的各次幂的系数分别为零，

$$
\ddot {x} _ {0} + \omega^ {2} x _ {0} = 0\tag{10}
$$

$$
\ddot {x} _ {1} + \omega^ {2} x _ {1} = x _ {0} ^ {2} - \alpha_ {1} x _ {0}\tag{11}
$$

$$
\ddot {x} _ {2} + \omega^ {2} x _ {2} = - \alpha_ {1} x _ {1} - \alpha_ {2} x _ {0} + 2 x _ {0} x _ {1}\tag{12}
$$

$x_0(t)$ 满足 $t = 0$ 时 $x_0 = A, \dot{x}_0 = 0$ 的解为

$$
x _ {0} (t) = A \cos \omega t\tag{13}
$$

将式(13)代入式(11)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {x} _ {1} + \omega^ {2} x _ {1} & = A ^ {2} \cos^ {2} \omega t - \alpha_ {1} A \cos \omega t \\ & = \frac {1}{2} A ^ {2} (1 + \cos 2 \omega t) - \alpha_ {1} A \cos \omega t \end{array}
$$

$x_{1}(t)$ 必须有限，要引起共振的项的系数必须为零，即 $\cos \omega t$ 项的系数必须为零， $\therefore \alpha_{1} = 0$ 

$$
\ddot {x} _ {1} + \omega^ {2} x _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} A ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} A ^ {2} \cos 2 \omega t\tag{14}
$$

作一级近似时， $\omega_0^2 = \omega^2 + \alpha_1 \lambda$ ，今 $\alpha_1 = 0$ ，有 $\omega^2 = \omega_0^2, \omega = \omega_0$ ，将式(14)中的 $\omega$ 改成 $\omega_0$ ，式(14)就变成式(7)，前已得到 $x_1(t)$ 满足 $t = 0$ 时 $x_1 = 0, x_1 = 0$ 的解式(8)，零级近似时 $\omega = \omega_0$ ，式(13)中的 $\omega$ 可改成 $\omega_0$ ，这样一级近似解与前面用通常的微扰法得到的完全相同。

下面求 $x_{2}(t)$ , 式(12)中的 $\omega^{2} \neq \omega_{0}^{2}$ , 代入 $x_{0}, x_{1}$ 时仍需保留原来的 $\omega$ , 不能用 $\omega_{0}$ , 代入 $x_{0}, x_{1}$ 和 $\alpha_{1} = 0$ , 式(12)变成

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {x} _ {2} + \omega^ {2} x _ {2} & = - \alpha_ {2} A \cos \omega t + 2 A \cos \omega t \left(- \frac {A ^ {2}}{3 \omega^ {2}} \cos \omega t + \frac {A ^ {2}}{2 \omega^ {2}} - \frac {A ^ {2}}{6 \omega^ {2}} \cos 2 \omega t\right) \\ & = - \alpha_ {2} A \cos \omega t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega^ {2}} (1 + \cos 2 \omega t) + \frac {A ^ {3}}{\omega^ {2}} \cos \omega t \\ & - \frac {A ^ {3}}{6 \omega^ {2}} (\cos 3 \omega t + \cos \omega t) \\ & = \left(- \alpha_ {2} A + \frac {5 A ^ {3}}{6 \omega^ {2}}\right) \cos \omega t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega^ {2}} (1 + \cos 2 \omega t) - \frac {A ^ {3}}{6 \omega^ {2}} \cos 3 \omega t \end{array}
$$

$x_{2}(t)$ 必须是有限的，不可能有出现共振的力项．由此， $\cos \omega t$ 的系数必须为零.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & - \alpha_ {2} A + \frac {5 A ^ {3}}{6 \omega^ {2}} = 0, \quad \alpha_ {2} = \frac {5 A ^ {2}}{6 \omega^ {2}} \\ & \ddot {x} _ {2} + \omega^ {2} x _ {2} = - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega^ {2}} - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega^ {2}} \cos 2 \omega t - \frac {A ^ {3}}{6 \omega^ {2}} \cos 3 \omega t \end{array}
$$

可得其特解为

$$
x _ {2} ^ {*} = - \frac {A ^ {3}}{3 \omega^ {4}} + \frac {2 A ^ {3}}{9 \omega^ {4}} \cos 2 \omega t + \frac {A ^ {3}}{4 8 \omega^ {4}} \cos 3 \omega t
$$

通解为

$$
x _ {2} = c _ {2 1} \cos \omega t + c _ {2 2} \sin \omega t + \frac {A ^ {3}}{1 4 4 \omega^ {4}} (- 4 8 + 3 2 \cos 2 \omega t + 3 \cos 3 \omega t)
$$

由 $t = 0$ 时 $x_{2} = 0, \dot{x}_{2} = 0$ 定 $c_{21}, c_{22}$ , 得

$$
x _ {2} = \frac {A ^ {3}}{1 4 4 \omega^ {4}} (- 4 8 + 1 3 \cos \omega t + 3 2 \cos 2 \omega t + 3 \cos 3 \omega t)
$$

二级近似解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} x (t) & = A \cos \omega t + \lambda \frac {A ^ {2}}{6 \omega^ {2}} (3 - 2 \cos \omega t - \cos 2 \omega t) \\ & + \lambda^ {2} \frac {A ^ {3}}{1 4 4 \omega^ {4}} (- 4 8 + 1 3 \cos \omega t + 3 2 \cos 2 \omega t + 3 \cos 3 \omega t) \end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega^2 = \omega_0^2 -\alpha_1\lambda -\alpha_2\lambda^2 = \omega_0^2 -\frac{5A^2}{6\omega_0^2}\lambda^2$ 

3.2.21 已知一个单摆的悬挂点做强迫竖直小振动 $\eta(t)=\eta_{0}\cos\omega t$ ，此单摆由长为 L 的无质量杆和杆端的质点组成。

(1) 假设单摆摆角振幅很小, $\eta_{0}\ll L$ ,求摆角 $\varphi$ 满足的运动微分方程;

(2) 求满足初始条件 t=0 时 $\varphi=a,\dot{\varphi}=0$ 的一级近似解；

(3) $w$ 为何值时发生共振, 写出共振时满足式(2)给的初始条件的一级近似解.

解 (1) 设质点质量为 $m$ ，取随悬挂点做平动的非惯性参考系，由对悬挂点的角动量定理

$$
m L ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} = - m g L \sin \varphi - m \ddot {\eta} L \sin \varphi
$$

$$
\eta = \eta_ {0} \cos \omega t, \sin \varphi \approx \varphi
$$

$\varphi$ 满足的微分方程为

$$
\ddot {\varphi} + \left(\frac {g}{L} - \frac {\eta_ {0} \omega^ {2}}{L} \mathrm{cos} \omega t\right) \varphi = 0\tag{1}
$$

(2) 令 $\lambda = \frac{\eta_0}{L} \ll 1, \frac{g}{L} = \omega_0^2$ 

$$
\varphi = \varphi_ {0} + \lambda \varphi_ {1}\tag{2}
$$

将式(2)代入式(1)，

$$
\ddot {\varphi} _ {0} + \lambda \ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + (\omega_ {0} ^ {2} - \lambda \omega^ {2} \cos \omega t) (\varphi_ {0} + \lambda \varphi_ {1}) = 0
$$

$$
\ddot {\varphi} _ {0} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \varphi_ {0} + \lambda (\ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \varphi_ {1} - \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {0} \cos \omega t) + \dots = 0
$$

$\lambda$ 的各次幂的系数分别为零

$$
\ddot {\varphi} _ {0} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \varphi_ {0} = 0\tag{3}
$$

$$
\ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \varphi_ {1} - \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {0} \cos \omega t = 0\tag{4}
$$

式(3)满足初始条件: t=0 时, $\varphi_{0}=a$ , $\dot{\varphi}_{0}=0$ 的解为

$$
\varphi_ {0} = a \cos \omega_ {0} t\tag{5}
$$

将式(5)代入式(4)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \varphi_ {1} & = \omega^ {2} \cdot a \cos \omega_ {0} t \cos \omega t \\ & = \frac {1}{2} a \omega^ {2} [ \cos (\omega + \omega_ {0}) t + \cos (\omega - \omega_ {0}) t ] \end{array}
$$

对两项非齐次项分别用振幅矢量法或待定系数法，可得其特解，进而写通解，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \varphi_ {1} & = c _ {1} \cos \omega_ {0} t + c _ {2} \sin \omega_ {0} t - \frac {a \omega}{2 (2 \omega_ {0} + \omega)} \cos (\omega + \omega_ {0}) t \\ & + \frac {a \omega}{2 (2 \omega_ {0} - \omega)} \cos (\omega - \omega_ {0}) t \end{array}
$$

满足 t=0 时， $\varphi_{1}=0,\dot{\varphi}_{1}=0$ 的解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \varphi_ {1} & = \frac {a \omega^ {2}}{\omega^ {2} - 4 \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \cos \omega_ {0} t - \frac {a \omega}{2 (2 \omega_ {0} + \omega)} \cos (\omega + \omega_ {0}) t \\ & + \frac {a \omega}{2 (2 \omega_ {0} - \omega)} \cos (\omega - \omega_ {0}) t \end{array}
$$

所以满足初始条件: t=0 时, $\varphi=a,\dot{\varphi}=0$ 的一级近似解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \varphi = & a \cos \omega_ {0} t + \frac {\eta_ {0}}{L} \left[ - \frac {a \omega^ {2}}{(2 \omega_ {0} + \omega) (2 \omega_ {0} - \omega)} \cos \omega_ {0} t \right. \\ & \left. - \frac {a \omega}{2 (2 \omega_ {0} + \omega)} \cos (\omega + \omega_ {0}) t + \frac {1}{2 (2 \omega_ {0} - \omega)} \cos (\omega - \omega_ {0}) t \right] \end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega_{0}=\sqrt{\frac{g}{L}}$ .

(3) 当 $\omega = 2\omega_0 = 2\sqrt{\frac{g}{L}}$ 时发生共振, 由于引起共振的是微扰项, 振动幅度仍然应是有限的, 需用处理长期项的办法.

将 $\omega = 2\omega_0$ 代入式(1)，仍用 $\lambda = \frac{\eta_0}{L}$ 

将

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {\varphi} + (\omega_ {0} ^ {2} - 4 \lambda \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \cos 2 \omega_ {0} t) \varphi & = 0 \\ \varphi & = \varphi_ {0} + \lambda \varphi_ {1}, \omega_ {0} ^ {2} = \omega^ {2} + \alpha_ {1} \lambda \end{array}
$$

代入上式, 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\ddot {\varphi} _ {0} + \lambda \ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + (\omega^ {2} + \alpha_ {1} \lambda) (\varphi_ {0} + \lambda \varphi_ {1}) - 4 \lambda (\omega^ {2} + \alpha_ {1} \lambda)} \\ & {\times \cos (2 \sqrt {\omega^ {2} + \alpha_ {1} \lambda t}) (\varphi_ {0} + \lambda \varphi_ {1}) = 0} \end{array}
$$

由 $\lambda$ 的各次幂的系数分别为零

$$
\ddot {\varphi} _ {0} + \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {0} = 0\tag{6}
$$

$$
\ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {1} + \alpha_ {1} \varphi_ {0} - 4 \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {0} \cos 2 \omega t = 0\tag{7}
$$

注意: 这里的 $\omega$ 已不是题中 $\eta(t)=\eta_{0}\cos\omega t$ 中的 $\omega$ , 这里 $\eta(t)=\eta_{0}\cos2\omega_{0}t$ , 而 $\omega\approx\omega_{0}$ .

式(6)满足 t=0 时， $\varphi_{0}=a,\dot{\varphi}_{0}=0$ 的解为

$$
\varphi_ {0} = a \cos \omega t\tag{8}
$$

将式(8)代入式(7)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {\varphi_ {1}} + \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {1} & = - \alpha_ {1} a \cos \omega t + 4 \omega^ {2} a \cos \omega t \cos 2 \omega t \\ & = - \alpha_ {1} a \cos \omega t + 2 \omega^ {2} a (\cos 3 \omega t + \cos \omega t) \\ & = (- \alpha_ {1} a + 2 \omega^ {2} a) \cos \omega t + 2 \omega^ {2} a \cos 3 \omega t \end{array}
$$

$\varphi_{1}(t)$ 是有限的，必须 $\cos \omega t$ 项的系数等于零，

$$
- \alpha_ {1} a + 2 \omega^ {2} a = 0, \quad \alpha_ {1} = 2 \omega^ {2}
$$

$$
\omega^ {2} = \omega_ {0} ^ {2} - \alpha_ {1} \lambda = \omega_ {0} ^ {2} - 2 \omega^ {2} \frac {\eta_ {0}}{L}
$$

$$
\omega^ {2} \left(1 + \frac {2 \eta_ {0}}{L}\right) = \omega_ {0} ^ {2}, \quad \omega^ {2} = \frac {\omega_ {0} ^ {2}}{1 + \frac {2 \eta_ {0}}{L}} = \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \left(1 - \frac {2 \eta_ {0}}{L}\right).
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \omega & = \sqrt {1 - \frac {2 \eta_ {0}}{L}} \omega_ {0} \approx \left(1 - \frac {\eta_ {0}}{L}\right) \omega_ {0} \\ & \ddot {\varphi} _ {1} + \omega^ {2} \varphi = 2 \omega^ {2} a \cos 3 \omega t \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

设上述非齐次方程的特解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \varphi_ {1} ^ {*} & = A \cos 3 \omega t \\ - 9 \omega^ {2} A + \omega^ {2} A & = 2 \omega^ {2} a. \quad A = - \frac {1}{4} a \end{array}
$$

式(9)的通解为

$$
\varphi_ {1} = c _ {1} \cos \omega t + c _ {2} \sin \omega t - \frac {1}{4} a \cos 3 \omega t
$$

满足 t=0 时， $\varphi_{1}=0,\dot{\varphi}_{1}=0$ 的解为

$$
\varphi_ {1} = \frac {1}{4} a (\cos \omega t - \cos 3 \omega t)
$$

$\varphi(t)$ 满足初始条件 $t = 0$ 时， $\varphi = a, \dot{\varphi} = 0$ 的一级近似解为

其中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \varphi = & \varphi_ {0} + \lambda \varphi_ {1} = \varphi_ {0} + \frac {\eta_ {0}}{L} \varphi_ {1} = a \cos \omega t + \frac {\eta_ {0}}{4 L} a (\cos \omega t - \cos 3 \omega t) \\ & \omega = \left(1 - \frac {\eta_ {0}}{L}\right) \omega \end{array}
$$

## 3.3 简谐波

## 3.3.1 一平面简谐波

$$
y = 0. 2 \sin [ \pi (0. 5 x - 3 0 t) ]
$$

x、y 的单位为厘米，t 的单位为秒．求波的振幅、波长、频率、波速以及 x=1cm 处质元振动的初相位．

解

$$
y = 0. 2 \sin [ \pi (0. 5 x - 3 0 t) ]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = 0. 2 \cos \left[ \frac {\pi}{2} - \pi (0. 5 x - 3 0 t) \right] \\ & = 0. 2 \cos \left[ 3 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{6 0}\right) + \frac {\pi}{2} \right] \end{array}
$$

与 $y = A\cos \left[\omega \left(t - \frac{x}{v}\right) + \alpha \right],\omega = 2\pi \nu$ 相比较，可得 $A = 0.2\mathrm{cm},\nu = 15\mathrm{s}^{-1},v = 60\mathrm{cm}\cdot \mathrm{s}^{-1},$ 

$$
\alpha = \frac {\pi}{2}, \lambda = \frac {v}{\nu} = \frac {6 0}{1 5} = 4 \mathrm{cm}
$$

$x = 1\mathrm{cm}$ 处质元振动的初相位为

$$
- \frac {3 0 \pi}{6 0} \times 1 + \frac {\pi}{2} = 0
$$

3.3.2 一波的频率为 $20s^{-1}$ ，波速为 $80m \cdot s^{-1}$ ，振幅为 0.02m，求：

(1) 波的相位相差 $45^{\circ}$ 的两个点间的距离；

(2) 在一给定点处, 时间相隔 0.01s 的两位移之相位差;

(3) 在一给定点处, 时间相隔 0.01s 的质元的两个位置的最大距离;

(4) 在一给定点处, 质元的振动相位相差 $45^{\circ}$ 的质元的两个位置的最大距离.

解 $y = 0.02\cos \left[40\pi \left(t - \frac{x}{80}\right)\right]$ 

(1) 

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x _ {1}}{8 0}\right) - 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x _ {2}}{8 0}\right) \right| = \frac {\pi}{4} \\ | x _ {2} - x _ {1} | = 0. 5 \mathrm{m} \end{array}
$$

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| 4 0 \pi \left(t _ {1} - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) - 4 0 \pi \left(t _ {2} - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) \right| \\ = 4 0 \pi | t _ {1} - t _ {2} | = 4 0 \pi \times 0. 0 1 = \frac {2}{5} \pi \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} y (x, t + 0. 0 1) - y (x, t) & = 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t + 0. 0 1 - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) \right] - 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) \right] \\ & = 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) + \frac {2}{5} \pi \right] + 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) - \pi \right] \end{array} \tag {3}
$$

可用振幅矢量法求合振动的振幅,即任一质元在时间相隔

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/677923f9f2666f27dba1fdd338835324968884f342cff4e021efbbf6513b8917.jpg)



图3.35


0.01s 的两个位置的最大距离.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {| y (x, t + 0. 0 1) - y (x, t) |} \\ & {\quad = 2 \times 0. 0 2 \sin \left(\frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {2}{5} \pi\right) = 0. 0 4 \sin \frac {\pi}{5} = 0. 0 2 3 5 m} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & (4) \left| y (x, t + \Delta t) - y (x, t) \right| \\ & = \left| 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t + \Delta t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) \right] - 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) \right] \right| \\ & = \left| 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) + \frac {\pi}{4} \right] + 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 4 0 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{8 0}\right) - \pi \right] \right| \\ & = 0. 0 4 \sin \frac {\pi}{\delta} = 0. 0 1 5 3 m \end{array} \tag {4}
$$

3.3.3 一列沿长弦线向 $x$ 正方向传播的平面简谐波，位于 $x = x_{1} = 0$ 及 $x = x_{2} = 1\mathrm{m}$ 处两质元的简谐振动如下：

$$
y _ {1} = 0. 2 \sin 3 \pi t, y _ {2} = 0. 2 \sin \left(3 \pi t + \frac {\pi}{8}\right)
$$

$y$ 的单位为米， $t$ 的单位为秒．求：

(1) 频率；

(2) 波长 $\lambda;$ 

(3) 波速 v;

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/cacc26453dc387414da614461c8963bedcf4e3362b6014d90ef1f919e553c0b3.jpg)


(4) 该两质元的相对速率的最大值.

图3.36

解 由 $x = x_{1} = 0$ 处质元的振动方程

$$
y _ {1} = 0. 2 \sin 3 \pi t
$$

可把此向 $x$ 正方向传播的平面简谐波表达为

$$
y = 0. 2 \sin \left[ 3 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) \right]
$$

由 $x = x_{2} = 1\mathrm{m}$ 处质元的振动方程

$$
y _ {2} = 0. 2 \sin \left(3 \pi t + \frac {\pi}{8}\right)
$$

可知

$$
\begin{array}{r l} - \frac {3 \pi \cdot 1}{v} & = \frac {\pi}{8} - 2 n \pi = - \frac {1 6 n - 1}{8} \pi \\ v & = \frac {2 4}{1 6 n - 1}, (n = 1, 2, 3, \dots) \\ w & = 3 \pi s ^ {- 1}, \nu = \frac {\omega}{2 \pi} = 1. 5 s ^ {- 1} \end{array}
$$

$$
\lambda = \frac {v}{\nu} = \frac {1 6}{1 6 n - 1} (\mathrm{m}) \quad (n = 1, 2, 3, \dots)
$$

$$
y _ {2} - y _ {1} = 0. 2 \sin \left(3 \pi t + \frac {\pi}{8}\right) - 0. 2 \sin^ {3} \pi t
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {y} _ {2} - \dot {y} _ {1} & = 0. 6 \pi \cos \left(3 \pi t + \frac {\pi}{8}\right) - 0. 6 \pi \cos 3 \pi t \\ & = 0. 6 \pi \cos \left(3 \pi t + \frac {\pi}{8}\right) + 0. 6 \pi \cos (3 \pi t - \pi) \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/c62da8e18af145fae781c8d44031a65527a0ac87c5b1810b77175221f77d03b6.jpg)



图3.37


$$
\begin{array}{r l} | \dot {y} _ {2} - \dot {y} _ {1} | _ {\max} & = 2 \times 0. 6 \pi \sin \left(\frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {\pi}{8}\right) \\ & = 1. 2 \pi \sin \frac {\pi}{1 6} = 0. 2 3 4 \mathrm{m/s}. \end{array}
$$

3.3.4 一平面简谐波以 $50\mathrm{m / s}$ 的速率沿 $x$ 负方向传播，在 $t = 1\mathrm{s}$ 时的波形如图3.37所示，写出此波的运动学方程，并画出 $t = 1\mathrm{s}$ 时各质元的 $\frac{\partial y}{\partial t} - x$ 图.

解 从图3.37上可看出，

$$
A = 1 0 \mathrm{mm} = 0. 0 1 \mathrm{m}, \lambda = 2 \times 1 0 = 2 0 \mathrm{m}
$$

已知 $v = 50\mathrm{m / s}$ 

$$
\nu = \frac {v}{\lambda} = 2. 5 \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}, \quad \omega = 2 \pi \nu = 5 \pi \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}
$$

波的运动学方程为

$y = 0.01\cos \left[5\pi \left(t + \frac{x}{50}\right) + \alpha \right],\alpha$ 待定. $t = 1s$ 时，在 $x = 0$ 处， $y = 0.005m, \frac{\partial y}{\partial t} > 0$ 

$$
0. 0 1 \cos (5 \pi + \alpha) = 0. 0 0 5
$$

$$
5 \pi + \alpha = \arccos \frac {0 . 0 0 5}{0 . 0 1} = \pm \frac {\pi}{3} + 2 n \pi
$$

因为

$$
\left.\frac{\partial y}{\partial t}\right|_{\substack{x = 0\\ t = 1}} = -0.05\pi \sin (5\pi +\alpha) > 0
$$

可见， $5\pi + \alpha$ 在第四象限

$$
5 \pi + \alpha = - \frac {\pi}{3} + 2 n \pi
$$

$$
\alpha = \frac {2 \pi}{3}
$$

此波的运动学方程为

$$
y = 0. 0 1 \cos \left[ 5 \pi \left(t + \frac {x}{5 0}\right) + \frac {2}{3} \pi \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial y}{\partial t} & = 0. 0 5 \pi \cos \left[ 5 \pi \left(t + \frac {x}{5 0}\right) + \frac {2}{3} \pi + \frac {1}{2} \pi \right] \\ & = 0. 0 5 \pi \cos \left[ 5 \pi \left(t + \frac {x}{5 0}\right) + \frac {7}{6} \pi \right] \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/0ecff908cd0fa3fec2658225ef04082028cf5a7a925695942ffa1e30ee3564f2.jpg)


$t = 1\mathrm{s}$ 时，各处质元的 $\frac{\partial y}{\partial t}$ 为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\partial y}{\partial t} & = 0. 0 5 \pi \cos \left(\frac {\pi}{1 0} x + 5 \pi + \frac {7}{6} \pi\right) \\ & = 0. 0 5 \pi \cos \left(\frac {\pi}{1 0} x + \frac {\pi}{6}\right) \end{array}
$$


图3.38


$\frac{\partial y}{\partial t} - x$ 的示意图如图3.38所示.

3.3.5 一根均匀的长弦线, 线密度为 0.02kg/m, 被 50N 的力拉紧, 弦线的一端 x=0

做简谐振动, 振幅为 0.02m, 周期为 1s, t=0 时, 位移 y 为 0.01m, $\frac{\partial y}{\partial t}$ 为负值. 写出向 x 正方向传播的简谐波的运动学方程.

解 $\eta = 0.02\mathrm{kg / m}, T_{0} = 50\mathrm{N}$ 

$$
A = 0. 0 2 \mathrm{m}, T = 1 \mathrm{s}, \omega = \frac {2 \pi}{T} = 2 \pi \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}
$$

$$
v = \sqrt {\frac {T _ {0}}{\eta}} = 5 0 \mathrm{ms} ^ {- 1}
$$

$$
y = 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 2 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{5 0}\right) + \alpha \right]
$$

$$
\frac {\partial y}{\partial t} = - 0. 0 2 \cdot 2 \pi \sin \left[ 2 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{5 0}\right) + \alpha \right]
$$

由 $t = 0$ 时 $x = 0$ 处 $y = 0.01\mathrm{m},\frac{\partial y}{\partial t} < 0$ ，可写出下列两式：

$$
\begin{array}{l} 0. 0 2 \cos \alpha = 0. 0 1 \\ - 0. 0 4 \pi \sin \alpha <   0 \end{array}
$$

$\alpha$ 在第一象限， $\alpha = \frac{\pi}{3}$ 

所以

$$
y = 0. 0 2 \cos \left[ 2 \pi \left(t - \frac {x}{5 0}\right) + \frac {\pi}{3} \right] \quad (\mathrm{m})
$$

3.3.6 一脉冲从长弦线的一端行进至另一端,需时0.1秒,弦线跨过一滑轮后悬一质量为弦线质量100倍的重物,问弦线的长度多大?

解 设重物质量为 m，弦线长度为 l，

$$
T _ {0} = m g, m = 1 0 0 \eta l, \eta = \frac {m}{1 0 0 l}
$$

$$
v = \sqrt {\frac {T _ {0}}{\eta}} = \sqrt {m g / \frac {m}{1 0 0 l}} = \sqrt {1 0 0 g l}
$$

$$
l = v t = \sqrt {1 0 0 g l} t, l ^ {2} = 1 0 0 g l \cdot t ^ {2}
$$

所以

$$
l = 1 0 0 g t ^ {2} = 1 0 0 \times 9. 8 \times (0. 1) ^ {2} = 9. 8 \mathrm{m}
$$

3.3.7 在拉紧的弦线上传播一个脉冲：

$$
y (x, t) = \frac {b ^ {3}}{b ^ {2} + (a x + u t) ^ {2}}
$$

其中 $a, b, u$ 均为正值常量，问：

(1) 此介质是色散介质吗？说明理由；

(2) 该脉冲速率多大？方向如何？

(3) 弦线上 x=0 处质元在 t=1 时的振动速度.

解 (1) 此介质是无色散介质, 对于色散介质, 不同频率的波在其中传播时有不同的波速. 一个脉冲是由频率连续变化的无数不同频率的简谐波叠加而成的, 由于不同频率的波波速不同, 随着时间的推移, 脉冲形状将发生变化. 而在无色散介质中传播, 脉冲的形状是不随时间变化的.

今 $t$ 时刻脉冲的形状为

$$
y (x, t) = \frac {b ^ {3}}{b ^ {2} + (a x + u t) ^ {2}}
$$

在 $t = 0$ 时，脉冲的形状为

$$
y (x, 0) = \frac {b ^ {3}}{b ^ {2} + (a x) ^ {2}}
$$

$x = 0$ 处，

$$
y (0, 0) = b, \quad x = \pm a, \quad y = \frac {b ^ {3}}{a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}
$$

在 $t$ 时，

$$
y (x, t) = \frac {b ^ {3}}{b ^ {2} + (a x + u t) ^ {2}}
$$

$x = -\frac{ut}{a}$ 处，

$$
y \left(- \frac {u t}{a}, t\right) = b = y (0, 0)
$$

$x = -\frac{ut}{a} \pm a$ 处，

$$
y \left(- \frac {u t}{a} \pm a, t\right) = \frac {b ^ {3}}{b ^ {2} + \left[ a \left(- \frac {u t}{a} \pm a\right) + u t \right] ^ {2}} = \frac {b ^ {3}}{a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} = y (\pm a, 0)
$$

同样,可以更一般的证明

$$
y \left(- \frac {u t}{a} \pm c, t\right) = y (\pm c, 0)
$$

这就说明了在传播过程中,脉冲的形状保持不变,介质是无色散的.群速 $u_{g}$ 与相速 $u_{p}$ 是相同的.

(2) 相速是相位的传播速度，

$$
a x + u t = \text {常量}
$$

$$
v = \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\mathrm{d} t} = - \frac {u}{a}
$$

负号说明脉冲是向 $x$ 负方向传播的，速率为 $\frac{u}{a}$ 。其实在(1)的解答中已能得出此结论。在 $t = 0$ 时，脉冲的极大值位于 $x = 0$ 处，在 $t$ 时刻，脉冲的极大值位于 $x = -\frac{u}{a} t$ 处，脉冲是以 $\frac{u}{a}$ 的速率向 $x$ 负方向传播的。

(3) 弦线上 x=0 处质元的振动速度为

$$
\left. \frac {\partial y (x , t)}{\partial t} \right| _ {x = 0} = \frac {\mathrm{d} y (0 , t)}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {b ^ {3}}{b ^ {2} + u ^ {2} t ^ {2}}\right) = - \frac {2 b ^ {3} u ^ {2} t}{(b ^ {2} + u ^ {2} t ^ {2}) ^ {2}}
$$

$t = 1$ 时，

$$
\left. \frac {\partial y (x , t)}{\partial t} \right| _ {x = 0} = - \frac {2 b ^ {3} u ^ {2}}{(b ^ {2} + u ^ {2}) ^ {2}}
$$

3.3.8 在两端固定、长度为 100m 的弦线上,有一如图 3.39 所示的脉冲以 40m/s 的速率向右行进,且保持其形状不变.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/8fadcd0cddf307a9d1519fefbbab9d32a4888a5f534f6a32c7bd17d6cace0e39.jpg)



图3.39


(1) 定性地画出在脉冲处于图示位置时弦线各质元的振动速度的轮廓图；

(2) 近似地估算弦线质元的最大振动速度；

(3) 若弦线总质量为 2kg, 弦线中的张力有多大?

解 (1) 在图示时刻, 弦线各质元的振动速度为

$$
\frac {\partial y}{\partial t} = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 0 \leqslant x \leqslant 3 9 \\ - v & 3 9 <   x \leqslant 4 0 \\ v & 4 0 <   x \leqslant 4 1 \\ 0 & x > 4 1 \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 v>0，轮廓图如图 3.40 所示。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/f3e38a971c70ab1db938bd56e5f8b8704ea79693aff1255b892aa3e6683e2232.jpg)



图3.40


(2)弦线质元最大振动速度就是(1)问解答中的 $v$ ，图3.41用实线和虚线分别表示 $t$ 时刻和 $t + \Delta t$ 时刻脉冲的位置。

脉冲传播的速率为 $40 \, m/s, AB = 40 \cdot \Delta t$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} A C & = v \Delta t \\ \Delta A B C & \sim \Delta A D E \quad \frac {A C}{A B} = \frac {A E}{D E} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/9a732feb30630e6750d08a807b665b5d8d5fa9d5eea78a6276d6910ef9f08ef0.jpg)



图3.41


即 $\frac{v\Delta t}{40\Delta t} = \frac{0.1}{1},\therefore v = 40\times 0.1 = 4\mathrm{m / s}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} v _ {\mathrm{g}} & = v _ {\mathrm{p}} = \sqrt {\frac {T _ {0}}{\eta}}, \eta = \frac {m}{l} \\ T _ {0} & = v _ {\mathrm{g}} ^ {2} \eta = v _ {\mathrm{g}} ^ {2} \frac {m}{l} = (4 0) ^ {2} \cdot \frac {2}{1 0 0} = 3 2 \mathrm{N} \end{array} \tag {3}
$$

3.3.9 一个对称的三角形脉冲在弦线上沿 $x$ 正方向运动, 其最大高度为 $0.4 \mathrm{~m}$ , 全长为 $1.0 \mathrm{~m}$ , 波速为 $24 \mathrm{~m} / \mathrm{s}$ , 在 $t = 0$ 时, 整个脉冲位于 $x = 0$ 至 $x = 1 \mathrm{~m}$ 之间. 画出 $x = 1 \mathrm{~m}$ 处质元振动速度随时间变化的曲线.

$$
\frac {\partial y}{\partial t} = \frac {0 . 4}{0 . 5} \times 2 4 = 1 9. 2 \mathrm{m/s}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial y}{\partial t} = - 1 9. 2 \mathrm{m/s}
$$

在 $t < 0$ 及 $t \geqslant 0.0417\mathrm{s}$ 期间

图3.43


$$
\frac {\partial y}{\partial t} = 0
$$

x=1m 处质元的振动速度随时间的变化曲线为不连续的四段直线,如图 3.43 所示.

3.3.10 气体或液体的压缩系数定义为 $k = -\frac{1}{\gamma} \frac{\mathrm{d}V}{\mathrm{d}p}$ , 这里 $-\mathrm{d}V$ 是压强增加 $\mathrm{d}p$ 时的体积减小量, 标准状态下空气的压缩系数比水约大 15000 倍.

(1) 推导声波速度公式 $v=\frac{1}{\sqrt{k\rho}}$ ，其中 $\rho$ 是质量

密度；

(2) 在标准状态下, 空气中的声速约为 $330 \mathrm{~m} / \mathrm{s}$ , 水中的声速约为 $1470 \mathrm{~m} / \mathrm{s}$ , 假设水是充满了微小气泡的混合物, 微小气泡仅占总体积的 $1 \%$ (忽略由气泡引起的混合物的密度和纯水的密度之间的差异), 求混合物的压缩系数 $k$ , 再求混合物中的声速 $v$ 值, 并与纯水或空气中的声速 $v$ 值相比较.

解 (1) 考虑一维问题, 考虑 $x \sim x + \mathrm{d}x$ 段质元, 传播声波时, 两端分别位移到 $x + y$ 和 $x + \mathrm{d}x + y + \mathrm{d}y$ , 两端压强分别为 $p_0 + (\mathrm{d}p)_x$ 和 $p_0 + (\mathrm{d}p)_{x + dx}$ , 其中 $p_0$ 是平衡时的压强, 设截面积为 $S$ .

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/c0ae92c9f7b09a11c1edb0e8c28f012d8e721255d15e4de457938d50108ecab4.jpg)



图3.44


该质元受到向右的力为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & [ p _ {0} + (\mathrm{d} p) _ {x} ] S - [ p _ {0} + (\mathrm{d} p) _ {x + \mathrm{d} x} ] S \\ & = - \frac {1}{k} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} V}{V}\right) _ {x} S - \left[ - \frac {1}{k} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} V}{V}\right) _ {x + \mathrm{d} x} \right] S \\ & = - \frac {1}{k} \left(\frac {\partial y}{\partial x}\right) _ {x} S + \frac {1}{k} \left(\frac {\partial y}{\partial x}\right) _ {x + \mathrm{d} x} S = \frac {1}{k} \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial x ^ {2}} S \mathrm{d} x \end{array}
$$

其中用了压缩系数的定义: $k=-\frac{1}{V}\frac{\mathrm{d}V}{\mathrm{d}p},\mathrm{d}p=-\frac{1}{k}\frac{\mathrm{d}V}{V}.$ 

该质元的加速度为 $\frac{\partial^2y}{\partial^2}$ ，由牛顿运动定律

$$
\rho S \mathrm{d} x \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial t ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{k} \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial x ^ {2}} S \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial x ^ {2}} - \rho k \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial t ^ {2}} = 0
$$

可见声波速度为

$$
v = \frac {1}{\sqrt {k \rho}}
$$

注意:这里定义的压缩系数是体积弹性模量的倒数.

(2) 对于混合物

$$
\begin{array}{r l} k & = - \frac {1}{V} \frac {\mathrm{d} V}{\mathrm{d} p} = - \frac {1}{V} \frac {\mathrm{d} V _ {1} + \mathrm{d} V _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} p} = - \frac {1}{V} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} V _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} p} + \frac {\mathrm{d} V _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} p}\right) \\ & = \frac {1}{V} \left[ V _ {1} \left(- \frac {1}{V _ {1}} \frac {\mathrm{d} V _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} p}\right) + V _ {2} \left(- \frac {1}{V _ {2}} \frac {\mathrm{d} V _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} p}\right) \right] = \frac {1}{V} (V _ {1} k _ {1} + V _ {2} k _ {2}) \end{array}
$$

设 $V_{1}$ 是混合物中气泡的体积, $V_{2}$ 为混合物中纯水的体积, $\frac{V_{1}}{V}=0.01$ , $\frac{V_{2}}{V}=0.99$ , $k_{1}=15000k_{2}$ .

$$
k = 0. 0 1 k _ {1} + 0. 9 9 k _ {2} = (0. 0 1 \times 1 5 0 0 0 + 0. 9 9 \times 1) k _ {2} = 1 5 1 k _ {2}
$$

在混合物中的声速

$$
v = \frac {1}{\sqrt {k \rho}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 5 1 k _ {2} \rho}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 5 1}} \times 1 4 7 0 = 1 2 0 \mathrm{m/s}
$$

比在水中的声速 1470m/s 和在标准状态下空气中的声速 330m/s 都小.

3.3.11 (1) 写出在弦中传播基波的频率与弦的物理性质、几何性质的关系；

(2) 从牛顿运动定律出发, 分析一小段弦所发生的变化导出上述关系.

解 (1) 设 $\nu$ 为在弦中传播的基波频率，

$$
\nu = \frac {1}{2 l} \sqrt {\frac {T}{\eta}}
$$

其中 l 为弦线长度, T 为弦的张力, $\eta$ 为弦的线密度.

(2) 考虑弦线位于 $x \sim x + \mathrm{d}x$ 段质元, 如图 3.45 所示, 受到的外力有 $T_{1}, T_{2}$ , 分别是 $x$ 左方和 $x + \mathrm{d}x$ 右方弦线对该质元的作用力, 重力与 $T_{1}$ 、 $\nu$ 

$T_{2}$ 的合力相比可忽略不计.

$$
T _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1} = T _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2} = T _ {0} \cos 0 ^ {\circ} = T _ {0}
$$

其中 $T_{0}$ 是弦线处于平衡时的张力.

在 $y$ 方向，质元所受的合力为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} - T _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} & = T _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2} \tan \theta_ {2} - T _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1} \tan \theta_ {1} \\ & = T _ {0} (\tan \theta_ {2} - \tan \theta_ {1}) \\ & = T _ {0} \left[ \left(\frac {\partial y}{\partial x}\right) \Big | _ {x + d x} - \left(\frac {\partial y}{\partial x}\right) \Big | _ {x} \right] \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/649fa50bf17cb538d09fe2ff4981fd62ec1136aa6cf4c0cdf8602749121a015f.jpg)



图3.45


$$
\approx T _ {0} \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial x ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

由牛顿运动定律

$$
\eta \mathrm{d} x \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial t ^ {2}} = T _ {0} \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial x ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x
$$

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial t ^ {2}} - \frac {T _ {0}}{\eta} \frac {\partial^ {2} y}{\partial x ^ {2}} = 0
$$

在弦线中传播波(只能是横波)的波速

$$
v = \sqrt {\frac {T _ {0}}{\eta}}
$$

在长度为 l 的弦线中传播频率最低的波(基波)的波长 $\lambda=2l$ ,

$$
v = \lambda \nu
$$

基波频率 $\nu = \frac{v}{\lambda} = \frac{1}{2l}\sqrt{\frac{T_0}{\eta}}$ ，即(1)中的 $\frac{1}{2l}\sqrt{\frac{T}{\eta}}$ .

3.3.12 声音在气体中的速度由下式计算: $v = \sqrt{\text{绝热体积模量/密度}}$ .

(1) 证明此公式的量纲是对的；

(2) 此公式隐含着认为声音在空气中的传播是准静态过程,但从另一方面,在空气分子的方均根速率大致为 500m/s 的温度下,空气中的声速大致为 340m/s,那么这种过程为什么能够是准静态的?

解 (1) 体积模量 $k$ 的定义为

$$
\Delta p = - k \frac {\Delta v}{v}
$$

准静态绝热过程的过程方程为 $pv^{\gamma}=c$ ，其中 $\gamma$ 是定压摩尔比热容与定容摩尔比热容之比，是无量纲的量，c 为常量。

可得准静态绝热体积弹性模量为 $k = \gamma p$ 。由此或直接由体积弹性模量的定义可见， $k$ 的量纲与压强的量纲相同。

$$
[ k ] = [ p ] = \mathrm{MLT} ^ {- 2} / \mathrm{L} ^ {2} = \mathrm{ML} ^ {- 1} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 2}
$$

$$
\sqrt {\frac {k}{\rho}} = \left(\frac {\mathrm{ML} ^ {- 1} \mathrm{T} ^ {- 2}}{\mathrm{ML} ^ {- 3}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} = \mathrm{LT} ^ {- 1} = [ v ]
$$

(2) 是否能用准静态过程近似, 要看恢复平衡所需的弛豫时间 $\tau$ 是否比此过程中外界变化经历的时间小得多. 今在空气中传播声波这个过程当然不是严格的平衡态, 但在局部可以看作近似处于平衡态. 声波频率从 $20\mathrm{Hz}$ 至 $20000\mathrm{Hz}$ , 就变化最快的 $\nu = 20000\mathrm{Hz}$ 的声波而言, 其周期 $T = 5 \times 10^{-5}\mathrm{s}$ , 在方均根速率为 $500\mathrm{ms}^{-1}$ 的温度下, 分子碰撞频率很高, 平均自由程大致为 $10^{-7}\mathrm{m}$ , 波长 $\lambda = \frac{v}{\nu} = \frac{340}{20000} = 1.7 \times 10^{-2}\mathrm{m}$ , 约为平均自由程的 $10^{5}$ 倍. 在发生传播声波的变化一个周期时间内发生了这么多次碰撞, 可以说, 恢复平衡的弛豫时间 $\tau \ll$ 声波的周期 $T$ , 因此可近似认为声音在空气中传播是个准静态过程.

3.3.13 若有下述两列波在介质中传播：

$$
y _ {1} = A \sin (5 x - 1 0 t), y _ {2} = A \sin (4 x - 9 t)
$$

式中 x、y 以米为单位，t 以秒为单位。

(1) 写出合成波的方程；

(2) 求群速度 $v_{g}$ ;

(3) 求出合成波中振幅为零的两相邻点间的距离；

(4) 说明此介质是否是色散介质,说明理由.

解 (1)

$$
y = y _ {1} + y _ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = A \sin (5 x - 1 0 t) + A \sin (4 x - 9 t) \\ = 2 A \sin \left[ \frac {1}{2} (9 x - 1 9 t) \right] \cos \left[ \frac {1}{2} (x - t) \right] \\ = 2 A \cos \left[ \frac {1}{2} (x - t) \right] \sin \left[ \frac {1}{2} (9 x - 1 9 t) \right] \end{array}
$$

(2) 群速 $v_{g}$ 是振幅的包络波

$$
2 A \cos \left[ \frac {1}{2} (t - x) \right]
$$

的传播速度, $v_{g}=1m/s$ .

(3) 合成波中振幅为零的两相邻点间的距离计算如下: 设同一时刻 t, 相邻的振幅为零的两点分别为 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ , 则

$$
\frac {1}{2} (x _ {1} - t) = \frac {\pi}{2}, \frac {1}{2} (x _ {2} - t) = \frac {3 \pi}{2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {1}{2} (x _ {2} - t) - \frac {1}{2} (x _ {1} - t) & = \frac {3 \pi}{2} - \frac {\pi}{2} = \pi \\ \Delta x & = x _ {2} - x _ {1} = 2 \pi \end{array}
$$

合成波振幅为零的点的位置是随时间变化的,可两相邻的振幅为零的点间距离不随时间变化.

（4）此介质是色散介质，因为在其中传播的两列波 $y_{1}, y_{2}$ 是具有不同频率的两列波： $\omega_{1} = 10s^{-1}, \omega_{2} = 9s^{-1}$ . 它们的传播速度不同； $v_{1} = \frac{10}{5} = 2ms^{-1}, v_{2} = \frac{9}{4} = 2.25ms^{-1}$ . 合成波的波形随时间发生变化．从(1)问所得的表达式也可作出此结论．

3.3.14 两个不同的音叉在完全相同的两段长绳上产生稳定的简谐波, 振幅 $A_{1} = 2A_{2}$ , 波长 $\lambda_{1} = \frac{1}{2}\lambda_{2}$ . 设绳子除了与音叉交换能量外, 不与其他物体交换能量. 求两音叉给予绳子的平均功率之比.

解 设绳子的截面积为 S，体密度为 $\rho$ ，线密度为 $\eta$ ，则 $\eta = \rho S$ .

$$
\overline {{{P}}} = I S = \frac {1}{2} \rho \omega^ {2} A ^ {2} v S = \frac {1}{2} \eta \omega^ {2} A ^ {2} v
$$

两绳完全相同意味着 $\eta$ 相同, 绳子张力 T 相同, 因而波的传播速度 v 相同.

今两音叉产生的两列简谐波,波长不同,而波速相同,可见频率是不同的:

$$
\frac {\omega_ {1}}{\omega_ {2}} = \frac {\nu_ {1}}{\nu_ {2}} = \frac {v / \lambda_ {1}}{v / \lambda_ {2}} = \frac {\lambda_ {2}}{\lambda_ {1}}
$$

$$
\frac {\overline {{{P}}} _ {1}}{\overline {{{P}}} _ {2}} = \frac {\frac {1}{2} \eta \omega_ {1} ^ {2} A _ {1} ^ {2} v}{\frac {1}{2} \eta \omega_ {2} ^ {2} A _ {2} ^ {2} v} = \left(\frac {\omega_ {1}}{\omega_ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {A _ {1}}{A _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} = \left(\frac {\lambda_ {2}}{\lambda_ {1}}\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {A _ {1}}{A _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} = 2 ^ {2} \cdot 2 ^ {2} = 1 6
$$

3.3.15 一正弦式空气波沿直径为 0.14m 的圆柱形管子的轴向传播, 波的强度为 $9.0 \times 10^{-3} J/m^{2} \cdot s$ , 频率为 300Hz, 波速为 300m/s, 求:

(1) 波中平均能量密度和最大能量密度；

(2) 每两个相邻质元同振动相位面间的区域中含有的机械能.

解 (1)

$$
\bar {\varepsilon} = \frac {I}{v} = \frac {9 . 0 \times 1 0 ^ {- 3}}{3 0 0} = 3. 0 \times 1 0 ^ {- 5} (\mathrm{J/m} ^ {3})
$$

最大能量密度 $\varepsilon_{max}=2\bar{\varepsilon}=6.0\times10^{-5}J/m^{3}$ 

(2) 相邻的质元振动同相位面间区域的总机械能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E & = \bar {\varepsilon} \cdot \frac {1}{4} \pi d ^ {2} \lambda = \bar {\varepsilon} \cdot \frac {1}{4} \pi d ^ {2} \frac {v}{\nu} \\ & = 3. 0 \times 1 0 ^ {- 5} \cdot \frac {1}{4} \pi (0. 1 4) ^ {2} \frac {3 0 0}{3 0 0} = 4. 6 2 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7} (\mathrm{J}) \end{array}
$$

3.3.16 一波源以 $3.5 \times 10^{4} \mathrm{~W}$ 的功率发射球面电磁波，在某处测得该波的平均能量密度为 $7.8 \times 10^{-15} \mathrm{~J} / \mathrm{m}^{3}$ . 求该处离波源的距离. 电磁波的传播速度为 $3.0 \times 10^{8} \mathrm{~m} / \mathrm{s}$ .

解 $\overline{P} = 4\pi r^2\bar{\varepsilon}(r)v$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} r & = \sqrt {\frac {\overline {{P}}}{4 \pi \bar {\epsilon} (r) v}} = \sqrt {\frac {3 . 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4}}{4 \pi \times 7 . 8 \times 1 0 ^ {- 1 5} \times 3 . 0 \times 1 0 ^ {8}}} \\ & = 3. 4 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} (\mathrm{m}) \end{array}
$$

3.3.17 两列平面简谐波

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & y _ {1} = A _ {1} \cos \left[ \omega_ {1} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] \\ & y _ {2} = A _ {2} \cos \left[ \omega_ {2} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \end{array}
$$

同时在同一介质中传播,分别讨论 $\omega_{1}\neq\omega_{2}$ 及 $\omega_{1}=\omega_{2}$ 两种情况下波强是否等于两波分别单独传播时的波强之和.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & y = y _ {1} + y _ {2} \\ & \quad = A _ {1} \cos \left[ \omega_ {1} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] + A _ {2} \cos \left[ \omega_ {2} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & \frac {\partial y}{\partial t} = - A _ {1} \omega_ {1} \sin \left[ \omega_ {1} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] - A _ {2} \omega_ {2} \sin \left[ \omega_ {2} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & \left(\frac {\partial y}{\partial t}\right) ^ {2} = A _ {1} ^ {2} \omega_ {1} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left[ \omega_ {1} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] + A _ {2} ^ {2} \omega_ {2} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left[ \omega_ {2} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & \quad + 2 A _ {1} A _ {2} \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \sin \left[ \omega_ {1} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] \sin \left[ \omega_ {2} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2} A _ {1} ^ {2} \omega_ {1} ^ {2} \left\{1 - \cos 2 \left[ \omega_ {1} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] \right\} \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2} A _ {2} ^ {2} \omega_ {2} ^ {2} \left\{1 - \cos 2 \left[ \omega_ {2} \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \right\} \\ & - A _ {1} A _ {2} \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \left\{\cos \left[ (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2}) (t - \frac {x}{v}) + \alpha_ {1} + \alpha_ {2} \right] - \cos [ (\omega_ {1} - \omega_ {2}) (t - \frac {x}{v}) + \alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2} ] \right\} \\ & = 0. 5 7 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 9 6 8 4 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3 3. \\ & = - y _ {1}, y _ {2}, y _ {3}, y _ {4}, y _ {5}, y _ {6}, y _ {7}, y _ {8}, y _ {9}, y _ {1 0}, y _ {1 1}, y _ {1 2}, y _ {1 3}, y _ {1 4}, y _ {1 5}, y _ {1 6}, y _ {1 7}, y _ {1 8}, y _ {1 9}, y _ {2 0}, y _ {2 1}, y _ {2 2}, y _ {2 3}, y _ {2 4}, y _ {2 5}, y _ {2 6}, y _ {2 7}, y _ {2 8}, y _ {2 9}, y _ {3 0}. \end{array}
$$

$\bar{\varepsilon} = \rho \frac{1}{\tau}\int_{0}^{\tau}\left(\frac{\partial y}{\partial t}\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t$ ，其中 $\pmb{\tau}$ 是 $T_{1}, T_{2}$ 的最小公倍数.

当 $\omega_{1} \neq \omega_{2}$ 时，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{\tau} \int_ {0} ^ {\tau} \cos 2 \Big [ \omega_ {1} \Big (t - \frac {x}{v} \Big) + \alpha_ {1} \Big ] \mathrm{d} t = 0} \\ & {\frac {1}{\tau} \int_ {0} ^ {\tau} \cos 2 \Big [ \omega_ {2} \Big (t - \frac {x}{v} \Big) + \alpha_ {2} \Big ] \mathrm{d} t = 0} \\ & {\frac {1}{\tau} \int_ {0} ^ {\tau} \cos \Big [ (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2}) \Big (t - \frac {x}{v} \Big) + \alpha_ {1} + \alpha_ {2} \Big ] \mathrm{d} t = 0} \\ & {\frac {1}{\tau} \int_ {0} ^ {\tau} \cos \Big [ (\omega_ {1} - \omega_ {2}) \Big (t - \frac {x}{v} \Big) + \alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2} \Big ] \mathrm{d} t = 0} \\ & {\bar {\epsilon} = \frac {1}{2} \rho A _ {1} ^ {2} \omega_ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \rho A _ {2} ^ {2} \omega_ {2} ^ {2}} \\ & {I = \bar {\epsilon} v = \frac {1}{2} \rho A _ {1} ^ {2} \omega_ {1} ^ {2} v + \frac {1}{2} \rho A _ {2} ^ {2} \omega_ {2} ^ {2} v = I _ {1} + I _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

当 $\omega_{1} = \omega_{2}$ 时，上述四个余弦函数的积分中，前三个仍为零，第四个不为零。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{\tau} \int_ {0} ^ {\tau} \cos (\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2}) \mathrm{d} t = \cos (\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2})} \\ & {\bar {\varepsilon} = \frac {1}{2} \rho A _ {1} ^ {2} \omega_ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \rho A _ {2} ^ {2} \omega_ {2} ^ {2} + A _ {1} A _ {2} \omega^ {2} \cos (\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2})} \\ & {I = \bar {\varepsilon} v = I _ {1} + I _ {2} + A _ {1} A _ {2} \omega^ {2} v \cos (\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2}) \neq I _ {1} + I _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

另一种计算法：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} y & = A _ {1} \cos \left[ \omega \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {1} \right] + A _ {2} \left[ \omega \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha_ {2} \right] \\ & = A \cos \left[ \omega \left(t - \frac {x}{v}\right) + \alpha \right] \end{array}
$$

由振幅矢量法(参看图 3.46)求 A,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} A & = \left[ A _ {1} ^ {2} + A _ {2} ^ {2} 2 A _ {1} A _ {2} \cos (\alpha_ {1} + \pi - \alpha_ {2}) \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ & = \left[ A _ {1} ^ {2} + A _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 A _ {1} A _ {2} \cos (\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2}) \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ I & = \frac {1}{2} \rho A ^ {2} \omega^ {2} v \\ & = I _ {1} + I _ {2} + A _ {1} A _ {2} \omega^ {2} v \cos (\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2}). \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/0673b713acda41fbb3b2140ca64cdb6647085d7863fd1dd221d85364f29334db.jpg)
