(1) 写出关于 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\alpha}{\mathrm{d}r}$ 的微分方程；

(2) 写出最接近距离 $r_{min}$ 所满足的方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/811fe7b28284b9e9dcd565416e4dc6b4f754cfe96e4e51d1b517edbadce451f5.jpg)



图4.14


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/011419843934070e2327e79ed1773f1934fc8d47e478ae3bd936a21a5ca263bf.jpg)



图4.15


解（1）由机械能守恒和对 $O$ 点的角动量守恒，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\alpha} ^ {2}) - G (r) = E _ {0}} \\ & {\qquad m r ^ {2} \dot {\alpha} = J} \\ & {\dot {r} ^ {2} = \frac {2 (E _ {0} + G)}{m} - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} r ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad \dot {r} = \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{\mathrm{d} \alpha} \dot {\alpha} = \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{\mathrm{d} \alpha} \frac {J}{m r ^ {2}}} \\ & {\left(\frac {\mathrm{d} r}{\mathrm{d} \alpha}\right) ^ {2} = \left(\frac {m r ^ {2}}{J}\right) ^ {2} \dot {r} ^ {2} = \left(\frac {m r ^ {2}}{J}\right) ^ {2} \left[ \frac {2 (E _ {Q} + G)}{m} - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} r ^ {2}} \right]} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} r}{\mathrm{d} \alpha} = \pm \frac {m r ^ {2}}{J} \sqrt {\frac {2 (E _ {Q} + G)}{m} - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} r ^ {2}}} = \pm \frac {1}{J} \sqrt {2 m (E _ {0} + G) r ^ {4} - J ^ {2} r ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\mathrm{d} \alpha}{\mathrm{d} r} = \pm \frac {J}{\sqrt {2 m (E + G) r ^ {4} - J ^ {2} r ^ {2}}}} \end{array}
$$

(2) 当 $r=r_{\min}$ 时, $\dot{r}=0$ ,

$$
\frac {2 \left[ E _ {0} + G (r _ {\min}) \right]}{m} - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} r _ {\min} ^ {2}} = 0
$$

4.3.4 一束很宽的平行小粒子束, 粒子质量为 m, 不带电, 从空间射向月球, 相对于月球的初速为 $v_{0}$ , 忽略地球和太阳的存在, 求用月球的半径 R、从月球表面的逃逸速度 $v_{esc}$ 和 $v_{0}$ 表示的碰击月球的碰撞截面 $\sigma$ .

解 求能碰上月球的最大瞄准距离 $\rho_{max}$ .

$$
\frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} - \frac {G M m}{R} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2}\tag{1}
$$

$$
m v R = m v _ {0} \rho_ {\max}\tag{2}
$$

其中 M 是月球质量, v 是刚能碰上月球的粒子碰月球时的速率, 刚能碰上月球的粒子是擦着月球表面相碰的.

由式(1)得

$$
v ^ {2} = v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 G M}{R} = v _ {0} ^ {2} + v _ {\mathrm{esc}} ^ {2}
$$

$\left( \text{后一等号来自} \frac{1}{2} mv_{\mathrm{esc}}^2 - \frac{GMm}{R} = 0 \right)$ 

将上式代入式(2)得

$$
\rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \frac {v ^ {2} R ^ {2}}{v _ {0} ^ {2}} = \left(1 + \frac {v _ {\mathrm{esc}} ^ {2}}{v _ {0} ^ {2}}\right) R ^ {2}
$$

碰击月球的碰撞截面为

$$
\sigma = \pi \rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \pi R ^ {2} \left(1 + \frac {v _ {\mathrm{esc}} ^ {2}}{v _ {0} ^ {2}}\right)
$$

4.3.5 一彗星以初速 $v_{0}$ 向太阳运动, 太阳的质量为 $M$ 、半径为 $R$ , 可视为静止的, 并略去所有其他的星体. 求彗星和太阳相撞的总截面.

解 如上题那样, 求彗星刚能碰上太阳的最大瞄准距离 $\rho_{max}$ , 设彗星质量为 m.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} - \frac {G M m}{R} & = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} \\ m v R & = m v _ {0} \rho_ {\max} \end{array}
$$

可得

$$
v ^ {2} = v _ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {2 G M}{R}
$$

$$
\rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \frac {v ^ {2} R ^ {2}}{v _ {0} ^ {2}} = \left(1 + \frac {2 G M}{R v _ {0} ^ {2}}\right) R ^ {2}
$$

$$
\sigma = \pi \rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \pi R ^ {2} \left(1 + \frac {2 G M}{R v _ {0} ^ {2}}\right)
$$

4.3.6 一个原子与一个离子的相互作用可由势能 $V(r) = -cr^{-4}$ 给出，其中 $c = \frac{e^2p_a^2}{2}$ ， $e$ 是离子电荷， $p_a$ 是原子的极化率。

(1) 作出有效势能 $U(r)$ 的草图；

(2) 求出用角动量 J 表达的有效势能的极大值 $U_{0}$ ;

(3) 设离子远比原子轻, 求离子击中原子(即深入到 $r = 0$ ) 的横截面 $\sigma(v_0)$ .

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U (r) & = V (r) + \frac {J ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} = \dot {c} r ^ {- 4} + \frac {J ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} \\ & = - c r ^ {- 4} + \frac {J ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

有效势能 $U(r)$ 的草图如图 4.16 所示.

(2) 

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} U}{\mathrm{d} r} = \frac {4 c}{r ^ {5}} - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m r ^ {3}}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} U}{\mathrm{d} r ^ {2}} = - \frac {2 0 c}{r ^ {6}} + \frac {3 J ^ {2}}{m r ^ {4}}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} U}{\mathrm{d} r} = 0, \quad \frac {4 c}{r ^ {5}} - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m r ^ {3}} = 0
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/f31ad8e95e12087c8382da4727545a6c122932ca39d53fe3d9fe98182c9df405.jpg)


解得


图4.16


$$
r _ {1} = \infty , r _ {2} = \frac {2 \sqrt {m c}}{J}
$$

$\left.\frac{d^{2}U}{dr^{2}}\right|_{r=r_{1}}=0,$ 还可看到 $\left.\frac{d^{3}U}{dr^{3}}\right|_{r=r_{1}}=0,\left.\frac{d^{4}U}{dr^{4}}\right|_{r=r_{1}}=0,\cdots.$ 在 $r\to\infty$ 时， $U(r)\to0$ ，不是极值。

$\left.\frac{d^{2}U}{dr^{2}}\right|_{r=r_{2}}=-\frac{7J^{6}}{16m^{3}c^{2}}<0. r=r_{2}$ 处，U有极大值。

$$
U _ {0} = U (r) \mid_ {r = \frac {2 \sqrt {m c}}{J}} = \frac {J ^ {4}}{1 6 m ^ {2} c}.
$$

(3) 设能量为 E、对力心的角动量为 J 的离子从无穷远处向原子入射，

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} + U (r) = E
$$

在 $r = r_{\min}$ 时， $\dot{r} = 0$ ，故 $r_{\min}$ 满足的方程为

$$
U (r) = E
$$

即

$$
- \frac {c}{r ^ {4}} + \frac {J ^ {2}}{2 m r ^ {2}} = E
$$

$$
2 m r ^ {4} E - J ^ {2} r ^ {2} + 2 m c = 0
$$

$$
r _ {\min} ^ {2} = \frac {J ^ {2} \pm \sqrt {J ^ {4} - 1 6 m ^ {2} c E}}{4 m E}
$$

当 $E = \frac{J^4}{16m^2c} = U_0$ 时， $r_{\min} = \sqrt{\frac{J^2}{4m \cdot \frac{J^4}{16m^2c}}} = \frac{2\sqrt{mc}}{J}$ ，不能击中原子；

当 $E < \frac{J^4}{16m^2c}$ 即 $E < U_0$ 时， $r_{\min} > \frac{2\sqrt{mc}}{J}$ ，这可从有效势能图上得出，在离子从无穷远入射时，上述 $r_{\min}^2$ 的式子，只能取 $r_{\min}^2 = \frac{J^2 + \sqrt{J^4 - 16m^2cE}}{4mE}$ ，

当 $E > \frac{J^4}{16m^2c}$ 即 $E > U_0$ 时， $r_{\min}$ 为复数。说明无 $r_{\min}$ ，从有效势能图可见，离子将深入到 $r = 0 (J = 0$ 时）或 $r \to 0 (J \neq 0$ 时），两种情况均可视为击中原子。

$$
J = m \rho v _ {0}, \quad \rho = \frac {J}{m v _ {0}}
$$

其中 $\rho$ 为离子在无穷远入射时的瞄准距离.

$$
E = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2}
$$

要击中原子, $E>U_{0}$ ,即 $E>\frac{J^{4}}{16m^{2}c}$ .

在无穷远处以 $v_{0}$ 入射的离子能击中原子的最大瞄准距离为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \rho_ {\max} (v _ {0}) & = \frac {J _ {\max}}{m v _ {0}} <   \frac {\left(\frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} \cdot 1 6 m ^ {2} c\right) ^ {\frac {1}{4}}}{m v _ {0}} = (8 m ^ {- 1} c v _ {0} ^ {- 2}) ^ {\frac {1}{4}} \\ \sigma (v _ {0}) & = \pi \rho_ {\max} ^ {2} (v _ {0}) <   \pi (8 m ^ {- 1} c v _ {0} ^ {- 2}) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} = \frac {2 \pi}{v _ {0}} \sqrt {\frac {2 c}{m}} \end{array}
$$

4.3.7 求一个粒子被半径为 $a$ 的刚球弹性散射的微分散射截面和总散射截面。

解 见图 4.17.

$$
\beta = \arcsin \left(\frac {\rho}{a}\right)
$$

$$
\theta = | \pi - 2 \beta | = \pi - 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {\rho}{a}\right)
$$

$$
\sigma (v, \theta) = \frac {\rho}{\sin \theta} \left| \frac {\mathrm{d} \rho}{\mathrm{d} \theta} \right|
$$

$$
\rho = a \sin \left(\frac {\pi - \theta}{2}\right)
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/42220e59113b4d44d2442e557732d2c86826d0e3b6a01c7bedaf3a5d9be260f1.jpg)



图4.17


$$
\begin{array}{r l} \sigma (v, \theta) & = \frac {a \sin \left(\frac {\pi - \theta}{2}\right)}{\sin \theta} \cdot \frac {1}{2} a \cos \left(\frac {\pi - \theta}{2}\right) = \frac {1}{4} a ^ {2} \sigma (v) = \int_ {0} ^ {\theta_ {\max}} \sigma (v, \theta) \cdot 2 \pi \mathrm{sim} \theta \mathrm{d} \theta \\ & = \frac {1}{4} a ^ {2} \cdot 2 \pi \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta = \pi a ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

4.3.8 一质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $r$ 的小弹子球被一质量为 $M(m \ll M)$ 、半径为 $R$ 的大弹子球弹性散射，求微分散射截面和总散射截面。

解 因为 $m \ll M, m$ 遭 $M$ 散射时可把 $M$ 视为不动的．和上题的差别在于上题的粒子的半径是不计的，弹性散射的情况和不计半径的 $m$ 被半径为 $R + r$ 的 $M$ 弹性散射一样．这样把上题的 $a$ 改为 $R + r$ 即得本题的结果．

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \sigma (v, \theta) & = \frac {1}{4} (R + r) ^ {2} \\ \sigma (v) & = \pi (R + r) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

4.3.9 质量为 $m$ 的粒子以速度 $v_{0}$ 从无穷远发射被大小为 $\frac{k}{r^2} (k$ 为常量)的固定的斥力中心所散射。如粒子不受斥力作用，粒子将以与斥力中心距离为 $b$ 的轨迹通过，求：

(1) 粒子接近斥力中心的最近距离；

(2) 实际产生的偏转角；

(3) 对于一均匀的粒子束受到该势场散射的微分散射截面 $\frac{d\sigma}{d\Omega}$ .

解 (1)

$$
f = \frac {k}{r ^ {2}}, \quad V = \frac {k}{r}, \quad h = b v _ {0},
$$

$$
U (r) = V (r) + \frac {m h ^ {2}}{2 r ^ {2}} = \frac {k}{r} + \frac {m b ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 r ^ {2}}
$$

$r_{\min}$ 满足的方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & U (r _ {\min}) = E _ {0} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} \\ & \frac {k}{r _ {\min}} + \frac {m b ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 r _ {\min} ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} \\ & r _ {\min} = \frac {1}{m v _ {0} ^ {2}} \left(k + \sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} b ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4}}\right) \end{array}
$$

$r_{\min}$ 的另一负根无物理意义,已舍去.

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{l} u _ {\max} = \frac {1}{r _ {\min}} = \frac {m v _ {0} ^ {2}}{k + \sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} b ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4}}} = \frac {- k + \sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} b ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4}}}{m b ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {2}} \\ \beta = \int_ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \frac {b d u}{\left[ 1 - \frac {1}{E _ {0}} V \left(\frac {1}{u}\right) - b ^ {2} u ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ = \int_ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \frac {b d u}{\left[ 1 - \frac {k}{\frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2}} u - b ^ {2} u ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ = \int_ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \frac {b d u}{\left[ 1 + \left(\frac {k}{m v _ {0} ^ {2} b}\right) ^ {2} - \left(b u + \frac {k}{m v _ {0} ^ {2} b}\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ = \arcsin \left[ \left(b u + \frac {k}{m v _ {0} ^ {2} b}\right) / \sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {k}{m v _ {0} ^ {2} b}\right) ^ {2}} \right] \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {u _ {\max}} \\ = \arcsin 1 - \arcsin \left(\frac {k}{\sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4} b ^ {2}}}\right) \end{array}
$$

偏转角

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\theta = \pi - 2 \beta = \pi - \left[ 2 \arcsin 1 - 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {k}{\sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4} b ^ {2}}}\right) \right]} \\ & {\qquad = 2 \arcsin \left(\frac {k}{\sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4} b ^ {2}}}\right)} \\ & {\qquad \sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2} = \frac {k ^ {2}}{k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4} b ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)}{1 - \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} = \frac {k ^ {2}}{\left(k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4} b ^ {2}\right) - k ^ {2}} = \frac {k ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4} b ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad b ^ {2} = \frac {k ^ {2}}{m ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {4}} \cot^ {2} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} \sigma}{\mathrm{d} \Omega} = \sigma (v, \theta) = \frac {b}{\sin \theta} \left| \frac {\mathrm{d} b}{\mathrm{d} \theta} \right| = \frac {1}{2 \sin \theta} \left| \frac {\mathrm{d} b ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \theta} \right| = \left[ \frac {k}{2 m v ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} \right] ^ {2}} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

4.3.10 (1) 质量为 $m$ 的粒子在势场 $V(r) = \frac{k}{r^2} (k > 0$ , 为常量) 中运动, 考虑在 $xy$ 平面内运动, 用极坐标 $r, \varphi$ , 求出 $r$ 作为 $\varphi$ 、角动量 $J$ 和能量 $E$ 的函数关系;

(2) 用(1)问中的结果讨论在这个势场中的散射, 求微分散射截面 $\sigma(\theta, E)$ , 其中 $\theta$ 为散射角.

解 (1)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & F = - \frac {\mathrm{d} V}{\mathrm{d} r} = \frac {2 k}{r ^ {3}} \\ & - m h ^ {2} u ^ {2} \left(\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} + u\right) = F = 2 k u ^ {3} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} + \left(1 + \frac {2 k}{m h ^ {2}}\right) u = 0 \end{array}
$$

令 $\omega^2 = 1 + \frac{2k}{mh^2}$ . 选取适当的极轴, 解可写为

$$
u = A \sin (\omega \varphi)
$$

由 $\varphi = 0, r = \infty$ 处， $E = \frac{1}{2} m \dot{r}^2 \bigg|_{\varphi = 0}$ 定 $A$ 

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {r} = \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} \dot {\varphi} = \frac {h}{r ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} = - h \frac {\mathrm{d} \left(\frac {1}{r}\right)}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} = - h \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} = - h A \omega \cos (\omega \varphi) \\ E = \left. \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} \right| _ {\varphi = 0} = \frac {1}{2} m h ^ {2} A ^ {2} \omega^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 m} J ^ {2} A ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \\ A = \frac {\sqrt {2 m E}}{J \omega} \\ r = \frac {1}{u} = \frac {J \omega}{\sqrt {2 m E}} \csc (\omega \varphi) \end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega = \sqrt{1 + \frac{2k}{mh^2}} = \sqrt{1 + \frac{2mk}{J^2}}.$ 

(2) 已解得

$$
u = \frac {\sqrt {2 m E}}{J \omega} \sin (\omega \varphi)
$$

如图4.18所示．在 $\varphi = 0$ 处入射，在 $\omega \varphi = \pi$ （此处 $u = 0, r = \infty$ ）出射，散射角 $\theta$ 为

$$
\theta = \varphi = \frac {\pi}{\omega} = \frac {\pi}{\sqrt {1 + \frac {2 k}{m h ^ {2}}}} = \frac {\pi}{\sqrt {1 + \frac {2 k}{m \rho^ {2} v ^ {2}}}} = \frac {\pi}{\sqrt {1 + \frac {k}{\rho^ {2} E}}}
$$

从上式解出

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\rho^ {2} = \frac {k \theta^ {2}}{E (\pi^ {2} - \theta^ {2})}} \\ & {\mathrm{d} \sigma = 2 \pi \rho | \mathrm{d} \rho | = \pi | \mathrm{d} \rho^ {2} | = \frac {2 \pi^ {3} k \theta}{E (\pi^ {2} - \theta^ {2}) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} \theta} \end{array}
$$

$\mathrm{d}\sigma$ 是具有能量 $E$ 的均匀粒子束被散射到 $\theta \sim \theta + \mathrm{d}\theta$ 所占的百分比.

$$
\sigma (\theta , E) = \frac {\mathrm{d} \sigma}{\mathrm{d} \Omega} = \frac {1}{2 \pi \sin \theta} \frac {\mathrm{d} \sigma}{\mathrm{d} \theta} = \frac {k \pi^ {2} \theta}{E \sin \theta (\pi^ {2} - \theta^ {2}) ^ {2}}.
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/85c111a57cde5e760ddf543f7bda68c0eb84fdf3b927425e95dd0259f66be075.jpg)



图4.18


它是具有能量 E 的均匀粒子束被散射到 $\theta$ 附近单位立体角内所占的百分比。

4.3.11 具有动能为 $T$ (非相对论) 的带电 $\pi$ 介子 ( $\pi^{+}$ 或 $\pi^{-}$ ) 和电荷为 $Ze$ 、有效半径为 $b$ 的重核。考虑经典力学的图像，当 $\pi$ 介子距核的最近距离等于或小于 $b$ 时， $\pi$ 介子就会打到核上。忽略核的反冲，证明 $\pi$ 介子的碰撞截面为

$$
\sigma = \pi b ^ {2} \Big (\frac {T - V}{T} \Big) \qquad \text { 对于 } \pi^ {+}
$$

$$
\sigma = \pi b ^ {2} \Big (\frac {T + V}{T} \Big) \qquad \text {对于} \pi^ {-}
$$

其中

$$
V = \frac {Z e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} b}.
$$

证明 对于 $\pi^{+}$ 介子，

$$
V (r) = \frac {Z e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}
$$

求能打中重核的最大瞄准距离 $\rho_{max}$ ，打中时， $r=r_{\min}=b$ ，设此时 $\pi^{+}$ 介子的速率为 v，由机械能守恒和角动量守恒，

$$
\frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} + \frac {Z e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} b} = E = T
$$

$$
v b = \rho_ {\max} \sqrt {\frac {2 T}{m}}
$$

解出

$$
\rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \frac {m}{2 T} v ^ {2} b ^ {2} = \frac {T - \frac {Z e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} b}}{T} b ^ {2} = \frac {T - V}{T} b ^ {2}
$$

其中 $V=\frac{Ze^{2}}{4\pi\varepsilon_{0}b}.$ 

$$
\sigma (E) = \sigma (T) = \pi \rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \pi b ^ {2} \left(\frac {T - V}{T}\right)
$$

对于 $\pi^{-}$ 介子，

$$
V (r) = - \frac {Z e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}
$$

用以上步骤,可得

$$
\sigma (E) = \sigma (T) = \pi b ^ {2} \left(\frac {T + V}{T}\right)
$$

其中 $V=\frac{Ze^{2}}{4\pi\varepsilon_{0}b}.$ 

4.3.12 在核物理中常遇到下列球形势阱：

$$
V (r) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} - V _ {0} & r \leqslant a \\ 0 & r > a \end{array} \right.
$$

(1) 证明: 在经典力学中被上述势产生的散射如同光线被相对折射率 $n = \sqrt{\frac{E + V_0}{E}}$ 、半径为 $a$ 的球的折射 (这等效说明为什么既能用惠更斯原理的波动说又可用牛顿力学的微粒说解释折射现象);

(2) 证明微分散射截面为

$$
\sigma (E, \theta) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {n ^ {2} a ^ {2}}{4 \cos \frac {\theta}{2}} \frac {(n \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - 1) (n - \cos \frac {\theta}{2})}{(1 + n ^ {2} - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}) ^ {2}} & 0 \leqslant \theta \leqslant 2 \arccos \frac {1}{n} \\ 0 & \theta \geqslant 2 \arccos \frac {1}{n} \end{array} \right.
$$

(3) 求总散射截面, 可能用到下列积分公式:

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\int \frac {x \mathrm{d} x}{(a + b x) ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{b ^ {2}} \Big [ \ln (a + b x) + \frac {a}{a + b x} \Big ]} \\ & {\int \frac {x ^ {2} \mathrm{d} x}{(a + b x) ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{b ^ {3}} \Big [ a + b x - 2 a \ln (a + b x) - \frac {a ^ {2}}{a + b x} \Big ]} \end{array}
$$

解 （1）图 4.19 和图 4.20 都是表示势阱的，从势能表达式看，表现为一个长方阱，而从空间位置上看，表现为一个球形阱。

质点从阱外向势阱入射时, 入射角设为 $\alpha_{1}$ (见图 4.20), 在进入势阱和离开势阱的两个边界上, 质点的势能都发生突变, 因机械能守恒, 因而动能也发生突变, 这说明质点受到力的作用, $F = -\nabla V$ , 方向沿该边界点与球心 $O$ 的连线指向势能减少的方向, 因而受到的力是有心力. 进入时, 动能增加, 受引力作用; 离开时, 动能减少, 受到的力也是引力, 在阱外和阱内均不受力的作用.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/a736fc2cd29a316445ce54aa12d33e49403f99554f45ec89a62ca33c2261eebd.jpg)



图4.19


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/372ed24f2a9a6b9065c16741ad9b83b613d7e4ebe692e2c8eb8d145fbfe96fec.jpg)



图4.20


设质点质量为 m，入射时速率为 $v_{1}$ ，入射角为 $\alpha_{1}$ ，进入势阱，在边界上，速率立即变为

$v_{2}$ ，设折射角为 $\alpha_{2}$ ，则：

由切线方向未受力，切线方向动量守恒，

$$
m v _ {1} \sin \alpha_ {1} = m v _ {2} \sin \alpha_ {2}
$$

由机械能守恒，

$$
\frac {1}{2} m v _ {1} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {2} ^ {2} - V _ {0} = E
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\sin \alpha_ {1}}{\sin \alpha_ {2}} = \frac {v _ {2}}{v _ {1}} = \frac {\sqrt {v _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {2}{m} V _ {0}}}{v _ {1}} = \sqrt {\frac {\frac {1}{2} m v _ {1} ^ {2} + V _ {0}}{\frac {1}{2} m v _ {1} ^ {2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {E + V _ {0}}{E}}
$$

根据光学中的折射定律，

$$
\frac {\sin \alpha_ {1}}{\sin \alpha_ {2}} = \frac {v _ {2}}{v _ {1}} = n
$$

$n$ 是相对折射率，

所以

$$
n = \sqrt {\frac {E + V _ {0}}{E}}
$$

这说明质点被这个势阱散射与光线按同样的入射角射入半径为 $\alpha$ 、相对折射率 $n = \sqrt{\frac{E + V_0}{E}}$ 的球的情况是可以类比的。

(2) 由图 4.20 可见, 散射角

$$
\theta = 2 \left(\alpha_ {1} - \alpha_ {2}\right)
$$

先求 $\rho (\theta)$ ，今有 $\rho = a\sin \alpha_{1}$ ，要将 $\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}$ 均表成 $\pmb{\rho}$ 和 $\theta$ 的函数，

$$
\frac {\sin \alpha^ {2}}{\sin \alpha_ {1}} = \frac {\sin \left(\alpha_ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \theta\right)}{\sin \alpha_ {1}} = \frac {\sin \alpha_ {1} \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - \cos \alpha_ {1} \sin \frac {\theta}{2}}{\sin \alpha_ {1}} = \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - \cot \alpha_ {1} \cdot \sin \frac {\theta}{2} = \frac {1}{n}
$$

从上式解出 $\cot \alpha_{1}$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\cot \alpha_ {1} = \frac {\cos \frac {\theta}{2} - \frac {1}{n}}{\sin \frac {\theta}{2}}} \\ & {\rho^ {2} = a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha_ {1} = \frac {a ^ {2}}{1 + \cot^ {2} \alpha_ {1}} = \frac {a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2}}{1 + \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} - \frac {2}{n} \cos \frac {\theta}{2}} = \frac {a ^ {2} n ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2}}{n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}}} \\ & {2 \rho d \rho = \frac {a ^ {2} n ^ {2} \left[ 2 \sin \frac {\theta}{2} \cos \frac {\theta}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{2} \left(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\right) - 2 n \sin \frac {\theta}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{2} \sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2} \right]}{\left(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\right) ^ {2}} d \theta} \\ & {\quad = \frac {a ^ {2} n ^ {2} \sin \frac {\theta}{2} \left(n \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - 1\right) \left(n - \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\right)}{\left(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\right) ^ {2}} d \theta} \end{array}
$$

$$
\sigma (E, \theta) = \frac {\rho}{\sin \theta} \left| \frac {\mathrm{d} \rho}{\mathrm{d} \theta} \right| = \frac {a ^ {2} n ^ {2}}{4 \cos \frac {\theta}{2}} \frac {\left| (n \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - 1) (n - \cos \frac {\theta}{2}) \right|}{(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}) ^ {2}}
$$

关于 $\theta$ 的取值范围：

当 $\rho = 0$ 时， $\alpha_{1} = 0, \alpha_{2} = 0, \theta = \theta_{\min} = 0.$ 

当 $\rho \to a$ ，时， $\alpha_{1}\rightarrow \frac{\pi}{2}$ ，由 $\cos \frac{\theta}{2} -\cot \alpha_1\sin \frac{\theta}{2} = \frac{1}{n}$ 

因为 $\cot \alpha_{1} \to 0, \cos \frac{\theta}{2} \to \frac{1}{n}, \theta_{\max} \to 2 \arccos \frac{1}{n}$ .

可见, $\theta < \theta_{\max}$ 时, $\cos \frac{\theta}{2} > \frac{1}{n}, n \cos \frac{\theta}{2} - 1 > 0$ . 在能发生的散射角范围内, 都有 $n \cos \frac{\theta}{2} - 1 > 0$ 的情况, 而在 $\theta > \theta_{\max}$ 即 $\theta > 2 \arccos \frac{1}{n}$ 时, $\sigma(E, \theta) = 0$ ,

$$
\sigma (E, \theta) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {a ^ {2} n ^ {2}}{4 \cos \frac {\theta}{2}} \cdot \frac {(n \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - 1) (n - \cos \frac {\theta}{2})}{(1 + n ^ {2} - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}) ^ {2}}, & 0 \leqslant \theta \leqslant 2 \arccos \frac {1}{n} \\ 0 & \theta \geqslant 2 \arccos \frac {1}{n} \end{array} \right.
$$

其中

$$
n = \sqrt {\frac {E + V _ {0}}{E}}
$$

(3) 

令

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\sigma (E) = \int_ {0} ^ {\theta_ {\max}} 2 \pi \sigma (E, \theta) \sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta} \\ & {\qquad = \int_ {0} ^ {2 \arccos \frac {1}{n}} \frac {a ^ {2} n ^ {2}}{4 \cos \frac {\theta}{2}} \frac {\left(n \cos \frac {\theta}{2} - 1\right) \left(n - \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\right)}{\left(1 + n ^ {2} - 2 n \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\right) ^ {2}} \cdot 2 \pi \cdot 2 \sin \frac {\theta}{2} \cos \frac {\theta}{2} \mathrm{d} \theta} \\ & {\cos \frac {\theta}{2} = x, \mathrm{d} x = - \frac {1}{2} \sin \frac {\theta}{2} \mathrm{d} \theta , \mathrm{d} \theta = - \frac {2 \mathrm{d} x}{\sin \frac {\theta}{2}}} \\ & {\sigma (E) = - \int_ {1} ^ {\frac {1}{n}} \frac {2 \pi a ^ {2} n ^ {2} (n x - 1) (n - x)}{(1 + n ^ {2} - 2 n x) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x = 2 \pi a ^ {2} n ^ {2} \int_ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1} \frac {(n ^ {2} + 1) x - n x ^ {2} - n}{(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x} \\ & {\qquad \int_ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1} \frac {(n ^ {2} + 1) x}{(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x = \frac {n ^ {2} + 1}{4 n ^ {2}} \Big [ \ln (n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x) + \frac {n ^ {2} + 1}{n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x} \Big ] \Big | _ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {n ^ {2} + 1}{4 n ^ {2}} \Big [ \ln \frac {(n - 1) ^ {2}}{n ^ {2} - 1} + \frac {n ^ {2} + 1}{(n - 1) ^ {2}} - \frac {n ^ {2} + 1}{n ^ {2} - 1} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {n ^ {2} + 1}{4 n ^ {2}} \Big [ \ln \frac {n - 1}{n + 1} + \frac {2 (n ^ {2} + 1)}{(n - 1) ^ {2} (n + 1)} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad \int_ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1} \frac {- n x ^ {2}}{(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {- n}{(- 2 n) ^ {3}} \left[ n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x - 2 (n ^ {2} + 1) \ln (n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x) - \frac {(n ^ {2} + 1) ^ {2}}{n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x} \right] \Bigg | _ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1} \\ = \frac {1}{8 n ^ {2}} \left[ - 2 (n - 1) - 2 (n ^ {2} + 1) \ln \frac {n - 1}{n + 1} - \frac {2 (n ^ {2} + 1) ^ {2}}{(n - 1) ^ {2} (n + 1)} \right] \\ \int_ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1} \frac {- n}{(n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} x = - \left. \frac {1}{2} \left[ \frac {1}{n ^ {2} + 1 - 2 n x} \right] \right| _ {\frac {1}{n}} ^ {1} = - \frac {1}{(n - 1) ^ {2} (n + 1)} \end{array}
$$

代入 $\sigma(E)$ 的式子, 经计算可得

$$
\sigma (E) = \pi a ^ {2}
$$

这个结果正是我们期待的,因为能产生散射的最大瞄准距离显然有 $\rho_{max} \rightarrow a$ ,

$$
\sigma (E) = \pi \rho_ {\max} ^ {2} = \pi a ^ {2}
$$

4.3.13 一束质量为 $m$ 、能量为 $E$ 的粒子垂直入射在厚度为 $d$ 、每单位体积内包含 $n$ 个固定散射中心的金属薄片上。一个粒子与一个散射中心间的力是大小为 $\frac{k}{r^2}$ ( $k$ 为常量) 的斥力。问束中粒子有多大部分被散射到 $\theta$ 大于 $\alpha$ 的立体角内？

解 一个散射中心对粒子的作用力与 4.3.9 题完全一样, 可用 4.3.9 题已解得的微分散射截面,

$$
\sigma (v, \theta) = \left[ \frac {k}{2 m v ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} \right] ^ {2}
$$

改成 $\sigma(E, \theta)$ . 因为 $E = \frac{1}{2} mv^2$ ,

$$
\sigma (E, \theta) = \left[ \frac {k}{4 E \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} \right] ^ {2}
$$

被一个散射中心散射到 $\theta >\alpha$ 的立体角内的粒子数所占的分数为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\int_ {a} ^ {\pi} 2 \pi \sigma (E, \theta) \sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta} \\ & {\quad = \int_ {a} ^ {\pi} \frac {2 \pi k ^ {2}}{1 6 E ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} \cdot 2 \sin \frac {\theta}{2} \cos \frac {\theta}{2} \mathrm{d} \theta} \\ & {\quad = \frac {\pi k ^ {2}}{4 E ^ {2}} \int_ {a} ^ {\pi} \frac {1}{\sin^ {3} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)} \cos \frac {\theta}{2} \mathrm{d} \theta = \frac {\pi k ^ {2}}{4 E ^ {2}} \cot^ {2} \left(\frac {\alpha}{2}\right)} \end{array}
$$

单位面积金属薄片内有 $nd$ 个散射中心, 能量为 $E$ 、垂直入射的粒子被金属薄片散射到 $\theta > \alpha$ 的立体角内的粒子所占的分数为

$$
\frac {n d \pi k ^ {2}}{4 E ^ {2}} \cot^ {2} \left(\frac {\alpha}{2}\right)
$$

如不是垂直入射, $\sigma(E,\theta)$ 没有差别,而与粒子束垂直的单位面积金属薄片内的散射中心数不再是nd.

4.3.14 一束质量为 $m$ 、能量为 $E$ 的粒子垂直入射在上题所述的金属薄片上，离粒子束通过薄片所在的 $O$ 点距离为 $r$ 的地方，放置一面积为 $A(A \ll r^2)$ 的粒子探测器，从 $O$ 点到探测器的线与粒子入射方向间夹角为 $\alpha$ (如图4.21所示), 若射到金属薄片上的粒子束流密度为 $I$ 、问单位时间内有多少粒子射中探测器?

解 考虑到 $A \ll r^2$ , 探测器对 $O$ 点所张的立体角很小, 可以忽略在这立体角内 $\sigma(E, \theta)$ 的差别.

用上题所得的结果(对于金属薄片单位面积内的 $nd$ 个散射中心)，

$$
\sigma (E, \theta) = \frac {n d k ^ {2}}{1 6 E ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {\theta}{2}\right)}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/4e79dad0bcdb4760981d64682cacda002b5c6ca1f02ad5defef7adcedeff9308.jpg)



图4.21


射到金属薄片上的粒子束流密度 $I$ 是单位时间内射到单位面积金属薄片上的粒子数.

单位时间内射到探测器上的粒子数为，

$$
N = I \sigma (E, \mathrm{d}) \mathrm{d} \Omega = \frac {I n d k ^ {2} A}{1 6 E ^ {2} r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {\alpha}{2}\right)}
$$

其中 $\mathrm{d}\Omega = \frac{A}{r^2}$ 是探测器所张的很小的立体角.

4.3.15 粒子束遇到由许多散射中心组成的靶子．设 $I(0)$ 是入射时的粒子束强度，进入靶后穿透 $x$ 距离处的强度为 $I(x)$ ．设束中一个粒子被一个散射中心散射的总散射截面为 $\sigma$ ，证明：

(1) $I(x)=I(0)e^{-n\sigma x},n$ 为单位体积内的散射中心数；

(2) 粒子被散射前运行的平均距离为 $\frac{1}{n\sigma}$ .

证明 (1) 一个粒子从 $x$ 到 $x + \mathrm{d}x$ 被散射的概率为 $n\sigma \mathrm{d}x$ ，强度减弱 $|\mathrm{d}I|$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \mathrm{d} I = - I n \sigma \mathrm{d} x \\ & \int_ {0} ^ {I (x)} \frac {\mathrm{d} I}{I} = - n \sigma \int_ {0} ^ {x} \mathrm{d} x \\ & \ln \frac {I (x)}{I (0)} = - n \sigma x \\ & I (x) = I (0) \mathrm{e} ^ {- n \sigma x} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

$$
\overline {{x}} = \frac {1}{I (0)} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} I (x) \mathrm{d} x = \frac {1}{I (0)} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} I (0) \mathrm{e} ^ {- n \sigma x} \mathrm{d} x = - \left. \frac {1}{n \sigma} \mathrm{e} ^ {- n \sigma x} \right| _ {0} ^ {\infty} = \frac {1}{n \sigma}
$$

## 第五章 刚体运动学

## 5.1 刚体上各点的速度和加速度

5.1.1 揉茶机的揉桶有三个曲柄支承，曲柄的支座为 $A, B, C$ ，支轴为 $a, b, c$ ，且 $A, B, C$ 与 $a, b, c$ 为两等边三角形，各曲柄长 $r$ ，均以不变的角速度 $\omega$ 绕其支座转动，如图5.1所示，求揉桶中心 $O$ 点的速率和加速度的大小。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/539a16af71b495378b8dc9b736fba989e08deb85e731aa1d79a1c1e334877268.jpg)



图5.1


解 揉茶机上的三个支轴 a、b、c 均有同样的速度, 刚体上不在一条直线上的三点有同样的速度, 刚体的运动是平动, O 点的速率等于 a、b、c 点的速率, O 点的加速度的大小等于 a、b、c 点的加速度的大小.

$$
v _ {0} = r \omega , \quad a _ {0} = r \omega^ {2}
$$

5.1.2 半径为 r 的半圆形凸轮以匀速 u 沿水平线向右运动, 带动直杆 AB 绕 A 点转动, 当 $\theta = 30^{\circ}$ 时, A 点与凸轮的中心 O 点正好在同一铅直线上(见图 5.2). 求此时 AB 杆与凸轮相接触的一点 C 的速度与加速度的大小.

解 以凸轮为动参考系, C 点对静参考系的速度是牵连速度与相对速度(沿 CA 方向)之矢量和, AB 绕 A 轴转动, C 点的绝对速度必垂直于 AC. 由此可得 C 点的速率

$$
v = u \cos \theta = u \cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} = \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} u
$$

要求此刻 C 点的加速度,须知道此刻 AB 杆的角速度 $\dot{\theta}$ 和角加速度 $\ddot{\theta}$ , 此刻,

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/8ebacab5abe86c8cfe3aeba5cdc0964672c171fbe288bcd9dd69bf2b4a941e9a.jpg)


$$
A C = \frac {r}{\tan 3 0 ^ {\circ}} = \sqrt {3} r
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} = \frac {v}{A C} = \frac {\frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} u}{\sqrt {3} r} = \frac {u}{2 r}
$$

图5.2

设图 5.2 所示位置时 t=0，图 5.3 画了 t 时刻的位形，

$$
A D = A O \mid_ {t = 0} = \frac {r}{\sin 3 0 ^ {\circ}} = 2 r
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} B D & = B H + H D = r + D O \cos \theta = r + u t \cos \theta \\ B D & = A D \sin \theta = 2 r \sin \theta \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/3c04cd88c68777ed721e13cb51626d3f458abad19627c12ea17785d5747e5306.jpg)



图5.3


所以

$$
r + u t \cos \theta = 2 r \sin \theta
$$

上式两边对 t 求导两次，

$$
\begin{array}{l} u \cos \theta - u t \sin \theta \dot {\theta} = 2 r \cos \theta \dot {\theta} \\ - u \sin \theta \dot {\theta} - u \sin \theta \dot {\theta} - u t \cos \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - u t \sin \theta \ddot {\theta} = - 2 r \sin \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 r \cos \theta \ddot {\theta} \\ - 2 u \sin \theta \dot {\theta} + (2 r \sin \theta - u t \cos \theta) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = (2 r \cos \theta + u t \sin \theta) \ddot {\theta} \end{array}
$$

代入 $t = 0$ 时的 $\theta = 30^{\circ},\dot{\theta} = \frac{u}{2r}$ ，得 $t = 0$ 时的 $\ddot{\theta}$ 为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \ddot {\theta} = \frac {1}{2 r \cos 3 0 ^ {\circ}} \left[ - 2 u \sin 3 0 ^ {\circ} \cdot \frac {u}{2 r} + 2 r \sin 3 0 ^ {\circ} \cdot \left(\frac {u}{2 r}\right) ^ {2} \right] = - \frac {u ^ {2}}{4 \sqrt {3} r ^ {2}} \\ a _ {n} = A C \Big | _ {t = 0} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \sqrt {3} r \cdot \left(\frac {u}{2 r}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {\sqrt {3} u ^ {2}}{4 r} \\ a _ {\tau} = A C \Big | _ {t = 0} \ddot {\theta} = \sqrt {3} r \left(- \frac {u ^ {2}}{4 \sqrt {3} r ^ {2}}\right) = - \frac {u ^ {2}}{4 r} \\ a = \sqrt {a _ {n} ^ {2} + a _ {\tau} ^ {2}} = \frac {u ^ {2}}{4 r} \sqrt {3 + (- 1) ^ {2}} = \frac {u ^ {2}}{2 r} \end{array}
$$

5.1.3 图 5.4 中画的是蒸汽机或内燃机中用的曲柄、滑块连杆机构。曲柄 OA 以等角速度 $\omega$ 绕 O 点转动，通过连杆 AB 带动活塞 B 在滑槽中左右运动。若 OA = R, AB = l, t = 0 时 $\varphi = 0$ 。求 t 时活塞 B 的速度。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/a331b1d23218bfa3e4aae1d4404bcc370d6c7fe8e16adc3d5797bb7b9efb3f9e.jpg)



图5.4


解 取 x 轴沿 OB 直线, 向左为正, O 为原点.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} x _ {B} & = - R \cos \varphi - l \cos \theta \\ & R \sin \varphi = l \sin \theta \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\mathrm{Reose}} \\ & {R \sin \varphi = l \sin \theta} \\ & {\sin \theta = \frac {R}{l} \sin \varphi , \quad \cos \theta = \sqrt {1 - \frac {R ^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} \sin^ {2} \varphi}} \\ & {\cos \dot {\theta} = \frac {R}{l} \cos \dot {\varphi} = \frac {R \omega}{l} \cos \varphi} \\ & {\dot {\theta} = \frac {R \omega}{l} \frac {\cos \varphi}{\cos \theta} = \frac {R \omega \cos \varphi}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - R ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi}}} \\ & {v _ {B} = \frac {\mathrm{d} x _ {B}}{\mathrm{d} t} = R \sin \dot {\varphi} + l \sin \theta \dot {\theta} = R \omega \sin \varphi + R \sin \varphi \cdot \frac {R \omega \cos \varphi}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - R ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi}}} \\ & {\varphi = \omega t} \\ & {v _ {B} = R \omega \left(1 + \frac {R \cos \omega t}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - R ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \omega t}}\right) \sin \omega t} \end{array}
$$

5.1.4 曲柄 $OA$ 以匀角速 $\omega_0 = 10\mathrm{s}^{-1}$ 顺时针方向转动(见图5.5). 求当 $\theta = 30^{\circ}$ 时 $BC$ 杆的角速度 $\omega$ 、角加速度 $\beta$ 及销钉 $A$ 在 $BC$ 杆导槽中的相对加速度 $a_r$ .

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/2482b01478febc29bd9a2a7f13083ba67e84f5377b76f354ffbeb865d16cb0eb.jpg)


$$
\begin{array}{r l} {\text {解}} & {2 \dot {\theta} = \omega_ {0}} \\ {\omega = \dot {\theta} = \frac {1}{2} \omega_ {0} = \frac {1}{2} \cdot 1 0 = 5 \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1} (\text {顺时针方向})} \\ & {\beta = \ddot {\theta} = 0} \end{array}
$$

下面求销钉 A 在 BC 杆导槽中的相对加速度．以 BC 杆为动参考系，


图5.5


$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {a} = \boldsymbol {a} _ {r} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {e} + 2 \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {v} _ {r} \\ \boldsymbol {a} _ {r} = \boldsymbol {a} - \boldsymbol {a} _ {e} - 2 \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {v} _ {r} \end{array}
$$

$a_{r}$ 沿 AC 方向, 设指向 C 的方向为正, 因为 BC 杆的角速度 $\omega \neq 0$ , 角加速度 $\beta = 0$ .

$$
a _ {e} = A C \cdot \omega^ {2} = 2 \cdot A O \cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} \times 5 ^ {2} = 8 6 6 (\mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2}),
$$

沿 BC 方向, 指向 C 点.

$$
a = A O \omega_ {0} ^ {2} = 2 0 \times 1 0 ^ {2} = 2 0 0 0 (\mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2})
$$

沿 AO 方向, 指向 O 点,

$2\omega\times v_{r}$ 在与 $a_{r}$ 相垂直的方向，

所以

$$
a _ {r} = a \cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} - a _ {e} = 2 0 0 0 \times \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} - 8 6 6 = 8 6 6 (\mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2})
$$

沿 $AC$ 方向，指向 $C$ 点.

5.1.5 摇床急用机构的曲柄以不变转速 $n = 90\mathrm{r / min}$ 绕定轴 $O$ 转动，通过滑块 $A$ 带动扇形齿轮绕 $O_{1}$ 轴转动，从而带动齿条 $B$ 做铅垂方向振动。设 $OO_{1}$ 在同一水平线上，曲柄 $OA = 76\mathrm{mm}$ ，其他尺寸如图5.6所示。

求 $\theta = 30^{\circ}$ 时齿条 $B$ 的速度和加速度.

解 设 $O_{1}A$ 与水平线的夹角为 $\varphi$ ,

$$
\dot {\theta} = - 2 \pi n / 6 0 = - 2 \pi \cdot 9 0 / 6 0 = - 3 \pi \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}
$$

$$
\tan \varphi = \frac {O A \sin \theta}{O O _ {1} - O A \cos \theta} = \frac {7 . 6 \sin \theta}{1 8 3 - 7 . 6 \cos \theta}\tag{1}
$$

$$
\sec^ {2} \varphi = 1 + \tan^ {2} \varphi = \frac {3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta}{(1 8 3 - 7 . 6 \cos \theta) ^ {2}}\tag{2}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/b24e0a1cecc8bb36c5e4ed6bf9732f84290a5f59b8338b84efd2d0ef356bff35.jpg)



图5.6


式(1)两边对 $t$ 求导，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \sec^ {2} \varphi \cdot \dot {\varphi} & = \frac {7 . 6 \cos \theta \dot {\theta} (1 8 3 - 7 . 6 \cos \theta) - (7 . 6 \sin \theta) ^ {2} \dot {\theta}}{(1 8 3 - 7 . 6 \cos \theta) ^ {2}} \\ & = \frac {- 5 7 . 8 + 1 . 3 9 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta}{(1 8 3 - 7 . 6 \cos \theta) ^ {2}} \dot {\theta} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

将式(2)及 $\dot{\theta}=-3\pi s^{-1}$ 代入式(3)，可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {\varphi} = - 3 \pi \frac {- 5 7 . 8 + 1 . 3 9 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta}{3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta} \\ \ddot {\varphi} = - 3 \pi \left[ \frac {- 1 . 3 9 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \sin \theta \dot {\theta} (3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta)}{(3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta) ^ {2}} \right. \\ \left. - \frac {(- 5 7 . 8 + 1 . 3 9 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta) \cdot 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \sin \theta \dot {\theta}}{(3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta) ^ {2}} \right] \\ = - 9 \pi^ {2} \frac {4 . 6 4 \times 1 0 ^ {7} \sin \theta}{(3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \cos \theta) ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

规定齿条向下的速度为正，

$$
v _ {B} = - 1 5 2 \dot {\varphi}, a _ {B} = - 1 5 2 \ddot {\varphi}
$$

代入 $\theta = 30^{\circ}$ 

$$
v _ {B} = - 1 5 2 (- 3 \pi) \frac {- 5 7 . 8 + 1 . 3 9 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \times 0 . 8 6 6}{3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \times 0 . 8 6 6} = 5 2. 8 (\mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1})
$$

$$
a _ {B} = - 1 5 2 (- 9 \pi^ {2}) \frac {4 . 6 4 \times 1 0 ^ {7} \times 0 . 5}{(3 . 3 5 \times 1 0 ^ {4} - 2 . 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {3} \times 0 . 8 6 6) ^ {2}} = 3 2 4 (\mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2})
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/ca0829a02a79fd7c34200bf5af8c60124ae7a99bdca4d3f8131b69d8e0af7847.jpg)



图5.7


5.1.6 图 5.7 中机构在竖直平面内运动, 各部件的尺寸如图所示. 某时刻在图示位置, 杆 OA 处于水平位置, 绕 O 点的角速度 $\Omega = 2 \, rad/s$ , 求此时部件 C 的角速度 $\omega$ 及杆 AB 的 B 端的速度的大小.

解 见解图5.8，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/e79e24446e167bc7af898d938bd9d9ec1f297b1ba64fa004447172c2e0b0ed3b.jpg)



图5.8


$$
\begin{array}{r l} v _ {A} & = \Omega \times O A = 2 \times 8 = 1 6 (\mathrm{cm/s}) \\ & = \frac {1 0 - 8}{1 4} = \frac {1}{7} \\ & = \arctan \frac {1}{7} = 8. 1 3 ^ {\circ} \\ & = 9 0 ^ {\circ} - 4 5 ^ {\circ} - \alpha = 3 6. 8 7 ^ {\circ} \end{array}
$$

考虑 AB 杆上的 A 点, 取部件 C 为动参考系,

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} = \boldsymbol {v} _ {A e} + \boldsymbol {v} _ {A r}\tag{1}
$$

$v_{Ae}=AO_{1}\cdot\omega=10\sqrt{2}\omega,\quad\omega$ 待定.

$v_{Ar}$ 沿 AB 方向， $v_{Ar}$ 待定。

由式(1)的水平和竖直两方向的分量方程，

$$
1 0 \sqrt {2} \omega \cos \alpha = v _ {A r} \cos \beta\tag{2}
$$

$$
1 0 \sqrt {2} \omega \sin \alpha + v _ {A r} \sin \beta = v _ {A} = 1 6\tag{3}
$$

式(2) $\times\sin\beta+$ 式(3) $\times\cos\beta$ 可消去 $v_{Ar}$ ，得

$$
1 0 \sqrt {2} \omega \sin (\alpha + \beta) = 1 6 \cos \beta ,
$$

$$
\omega = \frac {1 6 \cos 3 6 . 8 7 ^ {\circ}}{1 0 \sqrt {2} \sin 4 5 ^ {\circ}} = 1. 2 8 \mathrm{rad/s}.
$$

此时，AB杆围绕A点的角速度即部件C围绕 $O_{1}$ 点的角速度 $\omega$ .

取此时随 A 点平动的参考系为动系, B 点的绝对速度是 $v_{A}$ (牵连速度) 与 $v_{BA}$ (相对速度) 的矢量和, $v_{A}$ 与 $v_{BA}$ 的方向如图 5.9 所示,

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/3b5a364775bd6c2d9b43f61a3c5ce66806f2dcd04cf2a9be543d4427e9fdc9ff.jpg)


$$
\begin{array}{r l} v _ {B A} & = A B \cdot \omega = 2 0 \omega = 2 5. 6 \mathrm{cm/s} \\ v _ {B} & = \sqrt {(v _ {A} - v _ {B A} \cos \beta) ^ {2} + (v _ {B A} \sin \beta) ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

图5.9

代入 $v_{A}, v_{BA}$ 及 $\beta$ 值，可得

$$
v _ {B} = 1 6 \mathrm{cm/s}
$$

读者也可取部件 C 为动参考系, 求 B 点的绝对速度以检验上述结果.

5.1.7 若除上题所给条件以外, 还知道此时 $OA$ 杆绕 $O$ 点的角加速度 $\Omega = 0$ , 求此时部件 $C$ 的角加速度 $\dot{\omega}$ 及 $B$ 点的加速度 $a_B$ .

解 $A$ 点的绝对加速度为

$$
a _ {A} = O A \cdot \Omega^ {2} = 8 \times 2 ^ {2} = 3 2 (\mathrm{cm/s} ^ {2})
$$

沿水平方向，向左。

取部件 C 为动参考系， $a_{Ae1}$ 、 $a_{Ae2}$ 分别表示 A 点的牵连法向、切向加速度，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} a _ {A e 1} & = A D \omega^ {2} = 1 0 \sqrt {2} \times (1. 2 8) ^ {2} = 2 3. 1 7 (\mathrm{cm/s} ^ {2}) \\ & a _ {A e 2} = A D \dot {\omega} = 1 0 \sqrt {2} \dot {\omega} \end{array}
$$

这里已代入了上题解得的 $\omega$ 值. $a_{Ar}$ 是相对加速度, 必沿 AB 方向, 设图 5.10 所示的方向

为正方向.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/f359cb31c1d3a7244ab407c061ef5a2b8178086093548c0b87ae028aed44b0a2.jpg)


$$
\boldsymbol {a} _ {A C} = 2 \omega \times \boldsymbol {v} _ {A r}
$$

是科里奥利加速度,其中 $v_{Ar}$ 沿AB方向,指向B,由上题式(2)可得


图5.10


$$
\begin{array}{r l} v _ {A r} & = \frac {1 0 \sqrt {2} \omega \cos \alpha}{\cos \beta} = 2 2. 4 0 \mathrm{cm/s} \\ a _ {A} & = a _ {A r} + a _ {A e 1} + a _ {A e 2} + a _ {A C} \end{array}
$$

两个分量方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} a _ {A} & = a _ {A r} \cos \beta + a _ {A e 2} \cos \alpha - a _ {A e 1} \sin \alpha - 2 \omega v _ {A r} \sin \beta \\ a _ {A r} \sin \beta - a _ {A e 2} \sin \alpha - a _ {A e 1} \cos \alpha + 2 \omega v _ {A r} \cos \beta & = 0 \end{array}
$$

代入本题和上题已解得的数据,可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} a _ {A r} & = - 1 8. 1 7 \mathrm{cm/s} ^ {2} \\ \dot {\omega} & = 6. 0 2 \mathrm{rad/s} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

现在求 B 点的绝对加速度 $a_{B}$ ，仍取部件 C 为动系，

$$
\boldsymbol {a} _ {B} = \boldsymbol {a} _ {B r} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {B e 1} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {B e 2} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {B C}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/de4bb62a27ec648c378f968a25885f5e2d1f81e8e3cac61609f7240dd3fefd2f.jpg)


其中 $a_{Br}$ 、 $a_{Be1}$ 、 $a_{Be2}$ 、 $a_{BC}$ 的方向如图 5.11 所示，其大小分别为

$$
a _ {B r} = a _ {A r} = - 1 8. 1 7 \mathrm{cm/s} ^ {2}
$$

图5.11

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & a _ {B e 1} = a _ {A e 1} = 2 3. 1 7 \mathrm {cm/ s ^ {2}} \\ & a _ {B e 2} = a _ {A e 2} = 1 4. 1 4 \cdot 6. 0 2 = 8 5. 1 2 \mathrm {cm/ s ^ {2}} \\ & a _ {B C} = a _ {A C} = 2 \omega v _ {B r} = 2 \omega v _ {A r} = 5 7. 3 4 \mathrm {cm/ s ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

$$
a _ {B} = \sqrt {\left[ a _ {B r} + \left(a _ {B e 1} + a _ {B e 2}\right) \cos 4 5 ^ {\circ} \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \left(a _ {B e 2} - a _ {B e 1}\right) \cos 4 5 ^ {\circ} + a _ {B C} \right] ^ {2}} = 1 1 6. 8 \mathrm{cm} / \mathrm{s} ^ {2}.
$$

5.1.8 某瞬时刚体绕通过坐标原点的某轴转动, 刚体上一点 $M_{1}(1,0,1)$ 的速度 $v_{1} = 4\mathrm{m}\cdot \mathrm{s}^{-1}$ , 它与 $x$ 轴所成的角 $\alpha_{1} = 45^{\circ}$ ; 另一点 $M_{2}(3,4,0)$ 的速度 $v_{2}$ 与 $x$ 轴成 $\alpha_{2}$ 角, 且 $\cos \alpha_{2} = -0.8$ . 试求此刻刚体的角速度 $\omega$ 及 $M_{2}$ 点的速度的大小.

解 $\omega = \omega_{x}i + \omega_{y}j + \omega_{z}k$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {r} _ {1} = \pmb {i} + \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {1} = \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r} _ {1} = \omega_ {y} \pmb {i} + (\omega_ {z} - \omega_ {x}) \pmb {j} - \omega_ {y} \pmb {k}} \end{array}
$$

已知 $v_{1}=4\cos45^{\circ}i+v_{1y}j+v_{1z}k$ ,

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \omega_ {y} = 4 \cos 4 5 ^ {\circ} = 2 \sqrt {2} \\ & v _ {1 z} = - \omega_ {y} = - 2 \sqrt {2} \\ & v _ {1} ^ {2} = (2 \sqrt {2}) ^ {2} + v _ {1 y} ^ {2} + (- 2 \sqrt {2}) ^ {2} = 4 ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

解出

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & v _ {1 y} = 0 \\ & \omega_ {x} - \omega_ {x} = 0, \quad \omega_ {x} = \omega_ {x} \\ & r _ {2} = 3 i + 4 j \\ & v _ {2} = \omega \times r _ {2} = (\omega_ {x} i + 2 \sqrt {2} j + \omega_ {x} k) \times (3 i + 4 j) \\ & = - 4 \omega_ {x} i + 3 \omega_ {x} j + (4 \omega_ {x} - 6 \sqrt {2}) k \end{array}
$$

已知 $v_{2} = -0.8v_{2}\pmb {i} + v_{2y}\pmb {j} + v_{2z}\pmb{k},$ 

: 

$$
v _ {2 x} = - 0. 8 v _ {2} = - 4 \omega_ {x}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} v _ {2 y} & = 3 \omega_ {x}, \quad v _ {2 z} = 4 \omega_ {x} - 6 \sqrt {2} \\ v _ {2} ^ {2} & = v _ {2 x} ^ {2} + v _ {2 y} ^ {2} + v _ {2 z} ^ {2} = (- 4 \omega_ {x}) ^ {2} + (3 \omega_ {x}) ^ {2} + (4 \omega_ {x} - 6 \sqrt {2}) ^ {2} \\ & = 4 1 \omega_ {x} ^ {2} - 4 8 \sqrt {2} \omega_ {x} + 7 2 \\ v _ {2} & = \frac {4}{0 . 8} \omega_ {x} = 5 \omega_ {x} \end{array}
$$

由上述两式消去 $v_{2}$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & 1 6 \omega_ {x} ^ {2} - 4 8 \sqrt {2} \omega_ {x} + 7 2 = 0 \\ & 2 \omega_ {x} ^ {2} - 6 \sqrt {2} \omega_ {x} + 9 = 0 \\ & \omega_ {x} = \frac {6 \sqrt {2} \pm \sqrt {7 2 - 7 2}}{4} = \frac {3}{2} \sqrt {2} (\mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}) \\ & \omega_ {z} = \omega_ {x} = \frac {3}{2} \sqrt {2} \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1} \\ & \omega = \frac {3}{2} \sqrt {2} i + 2 \sqrt {2} j + \frac {3}{2} \sqrt {2} k \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \omega & = \sqrt {\left(\frac {3}{2} \sqrt {2}\right) ^ {2} + (2 \sqrt {2}) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {3}{2} \sqrt {2}\right) ^ {2}} = \sqrt {1 7} \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1} \\ v _ {2} & = 5 \omega_ {x} = \frac {1 5}{2} \sqrt {2} \mathrm{m} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1} \end{array}
$$

5.1.9 半径为 $b$ 的圆筒在半径为 $a$ 的固定圆柱上做纯滚动。若相切的直线从静止开始以恒定的角加速度 $\beta$ 围绕固定圆柱的轴顺时针转动，图5.12中画的是 $t = 0$ 时的位置。求圆筒上 $P, C$ 两点 $t$ 时刻的速度和加速度（均按圆筒的切向和法向分解）。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/22529f6d9337d9a78b66ec929a24fcdab5a7c66e5afce55bfb6279df399098d2.jpg)



图5.12


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/4a1766fa05a17a8ed3227b20f64a3b17608ae20be9ad927d64b0b22507bf0725.jpg)



图5.13


解 图 5.13 中用实线表示 t 时刻圆筒的位置, 虚线表示 t=0 时刻圆筒的位置. 取图中的 xy 坐标, 原点位于固定圆柱的轴上.

$t = 0$ 时相切的直线位于 $\theta = 0$ ，由题设， $\dot{\theta} = 0, \dot{\theta} = \beta (\dot{\theta}$ 恒定）在 $t$ 时刻， $\dot{\theta} = \beta t, \theta = \frac{1}{2}\beta t^2.$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {x _ {C} = - (b - a) \sin \theta , y _ {C} = - (b - a) \cos \theta} \\ & {\dot {x} _ {C} = - (b - a) \cos \theta \dot {\theta} = - (b - a) \beta t \cos \theta} \\ & {\ddot {x} _ {C} = - (b - a) \beta \cos \theta + (b - a) \dot {\beta} ^ {2} t ^ {2} \sin \theta} \\ & {\qquad \dot {y} _ {C} = (b - a) \beta t \sin \theta} \\ & {\ddot {y} _ {C} = (b - a) \beta \sin \theta + (b - a) \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \cos \theta} \\ & {v _ {C t} = - \dot {x} _ {C} \cos \theta + \dot {y} _ {C} \sin \theta = (b - a) \beta t} \\ & {\qquad v _ {C n} = \dot {x} _ {C} \sin \theta + \dot {y} _ {C} \cos \theta = 0} \\ & {a _ {C t} = - \ddot {x} _ {C} \cos \theta + \ddot {y} _ {C} \sin \theta = (b - a) \beta} \\ & {a _ {C n} = \ddot {x} _ {C} \sin \theta + \ddot {y} _ {C} \cos \theta = (b - a) \beta^ {2} t ^ {2}} \\ & {\widehat {P Q} = a \theta , \quad \therefore \angle Q C P = \frac {a}{b} \theta} \end{array}
$$

t 时刻 CP 与 -y 轴的夹角为 $\theta - \frac{a}{b}\theta = \frac{b - a}{b}\theta$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {x _ {P} = - (b - a) \sin \theta - b \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {y _ {P} = - (b - a) \cos \theta - b \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {\dot {x} _ {P} = - (b - a) \beta t \cos \theta - (b - a) \beta t \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {\ddot {x} _ {P} = - (b - a) \beta \cos \theta + (b - a) \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \sin \theta} \\ & {- (b - a) \beta \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right) + \frac {(b - a) ^ {2}}{b} \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {\dot {y} _ {P} = (b - a) \beta t \sin \theta + (b - a) \beta t \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {\ddot {y} _ {P} = (b - a) \beta \sin \theta + (b - a) \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \cos \theta} \\ & {+ (b - a) \beta \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right) + \frac {(b - a) ^ {2}}{b} \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {v _ {P t} = - \dot {x} _ {P} \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right) + \dot {y} _ {P} \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {= (b - a) \beta t \left[ 1 + \cos \left(\frac {a}{b} \theta\right) \right]} \\ & {v _ {P n} = \dot {x} _ {P} \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right) + \dot {y} _ {P} \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right) = (b - a) \beta t \sin \left(\frac {a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {a _ {P t} = - \ddot {x} _ {P} \cos \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right) + \ddot {y} _ {P} \sin \left(\frac {b - a}{b} \theta\right)} \\ & {= (b - a) \beta [ 1 + \cos (\frac {a}{b} \theta) ] - (b - a) \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \sin (\frac {a}{b} \theta)} \\ & {a _ {P n} = \ddot {x} _ {P} \sin (\frac {b - a}{b} \theta) + \ddot {y} _ {P} \cos (\frac {b - a}{b} \theta)} \\ & {= - (b - a) \beta \sin (\frac {a}{b} \theta) + (b - a) \beta^ {2} t ^ {2} \cos (\frac {a}{b} \theta) + \frac {(b - a) ^ {2}}{b} \beta^ {2} t ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

其中 $\theta = \frac{1}{2}\beta t^2.$ 

5.1.10 证明刚体上任何两点的速度在它们连线上的投影相等(速度投影定理).

证明 刚体上任意两点 A、B，它们对刚体上另一点 C（不一定是质心）的位矢为 $r_{A}$ 和 $r_{B}$ .

今取此 $C$ 点为基点，刚体的运动可分为跟随基点的平动和围绕过此基点的轴的转动。设此时刚体的角速度为 $\omega$ ，基点 $C$ 的速度为 $\pmb{v}_{C}$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {A} = \pmb {v} _ {C} + \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r} _ {A}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {B} = \pmb {v} _ {C} + \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r} _ {B}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {A} - \pmb {v} _ {B} = \pmb {\omega} \times (\pmb {r} _ {A} - \pmb {r} _ {B})} \\ & {(\pmb {v} _ {A} - \pmb {v} _ {B}) \cdot \frac {\pmb {r} _ {A} - \pmb {r} _ {B}}{| \pmb {r} _ {A} - \pmb {r} _ {B} |} = [ \pmb {\omega} \times (\pmb {r} _ {A} - \pmb {r} _ {B}) ] \cdot \frac {\pmb {r} _ {A} - \pmb {r} _ {B}}{| \pmb {r} _ {A} - \pmb {r} _ {B} |} = 0} \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} \cdot \frac {\boldsymbol {r} _ {A} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {B}}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {A} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {B} |} = \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} \cdot \frac {\boldsymbol {r} _ {A} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {B}}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {A} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {B} |}
$$

5.1.11 曲柄 $OA$ 在图 5.14 所示位置 ( $OA$ 与 $DC$ 的夹角为 $60^{\circ}$ . $BD$ 与 $OA$ 垂直) 绕定轴 $O$ 转动的角速度 $\omega = 4\mathrm{s}^{-1}$ , $OA = AB = AD = a = 30\mathrm{cm}$ , $BC = b = 40\mathrm{cm}$ , 滑块 $C, D$ 在同一水平直线上, 并限在水平滑道内滑动, 求此时刻滑块 $C, D$ 的速度.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/a5accd9c2b958d41633d5416970bc257f75f0ba1897a10c8d7cdabcc1a196570.jpg)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/ea02b5f6d27f09e44e279c5e5cdbf7a94c6b252d1d5291b78c076d9ba673dd02.jpg)



图5.14



图5.15


解 BD 做平面平行运动, 已知在此时 BD 上 D、A 两点的速度方向, 如图 5.15 所示, 由 D、A 两点分别作 $v_{D}$ 、 $v_{A}$ 的垂线, 其交点 G 是此时杆 BD 的瞬心.

显见， $BG = DG = 2a, GA = \sqrt{3} a, v_B$ 与 $DB$ 的夹角为 $30^{\circ}$ ，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/474e65e94f6f0e2449b582ef7e14b33607d4c61fae014d12e08cb4c34f407117.jpg)


$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} = a \omega = 3 0 \cdot 4 = 1 2 0 (\mathrm{cm/s})
$$

图5.16

用速度投影定理，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & v _ {D} \cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} = v _ {B} \cos 3 0 ^ {\circ} = v _ {A} = 1 2 0 \\ & v _ {D} = v _ {B} = \frac {1 2 0}{\cos 3 0 ^ {\circ}} = 1 3 9 (\mathrm{cm/s}) \end{array}
$$

对 $\Delta BCD$ 用正弦定理，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {b}{\sin 3 0 ^ {\circ}} = \frac {2 a}{\sin \angle C}} \\ & {\sin \angle C = \frac {2 a}{b} \sin 3 0 ^ {\circ} = \frac {2 \times 3 0}{4 0} \times 0. 5 = 0. 7 5} \\ & {\angle C = \arcsin 0. 7 5 = 4 8. 6 ^ {\circ}} \\ & {\angle B = 1 8 0 ^ {\circ} - 3 0 ^ {\circ} - 4 8. 6 ^ {\circ} = 1 0 1. 4 ^ {\circ}} \end{array}
$$

再对 $BC$ 杆用速度投影定理，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & v _ {C} \cos \angle C = v _ {B} \cos (\angle B - 3 0 ^ {\circ}) \\ & v _ {C} = \frac {v _ {B} \cos (\angle B - 3 0 ^ {\circ})}{\cos \angle C} = \frac {1 3 9 \cos 7 1 . 4 ^ {\circ}}{\cos 4 8 . 6 ^ {\circ}} = 6 7. 0 (\mathrm{cm/s}) \end{array}
$$

方向沿 $CD$ 指向 $D$ .

5.1.12 一个半径为 $r$ 的圆柱沿一平面做无滑滚动，其轴线 $O$ 保持速度 $v_{0}$ 不变，一块长度为 $l$ 的矩形平板其 $B$ 边以铰链接在圆柱的边缘上，另一端 $A$ 边搁在上述平面上随圆柱运动。若 $t = 0$ 时 $\varphi = 0$ ，求 $A$ 边的速度与时间的关系，并取适当的坐标，取最方便的基点，写出 $AB$ 板的运动学方程。

解 取 $xy$ 坐标, 原点取在 $t = 0$ 时 $B$ 边所在位置, $x$ 轴沿 $\pmb{v}_0$ 的方向, $y$ 轴竖直向上,

$$
x _ {A} = x _ {D} - A D = x _ {O} - A D
$$

$$
v _ {A} = \frac {\mathrm{d} x _ {O}}{\mathrm{d} t} - \frac {\mathrm{d} A D}{\mathrm{d} t} = v _ {0} - \frac {\mathrm{d} A D}{\mathrm{d} t}
$$

$$
A D = l \cos \theta + r \sin \varphi
$$

$$
l \sin \theta = r (1 - \cos \varphi)
$$

$$
\sin \theta = \frac {r}{l} (1 - \cos \varphi)
$$

$$
\cos \theta = \sqrt {1 - \sin^ {2} \theta} = \frac {1}{l} \sqrt {l ^ {2} - r ^ {2} (l - \cos \varphi) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
A D = \sqrt {l ^ {2} - r ^ {2} (1 - \cos \varphi) ^ {2}} + r \sin \varphi
$$

$$
v _ {A} = v _ {0} - \left[ \frac {- 2 r ^ {2} (1 - \cos \varphi)}{2 \sqrt {l ^ {2} - r ^ {2} (1 - \cos \varphi) ^ {2}}} \sin \varphi \cdot \dot {\varphi} + r \cos \varphi \dot {\varphi} \right]
$$

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {v _ {0}}{r}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} v _ {A} & = v _ {0} + \frac {v _ {0} r (1 - \cos \varphi) \sin \varphi}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - r ^ {2} (1 - \cos \varphi) ^ {2}}} - v _ {0} \cos \varphi \\ & = v _ {0} (1 - \cos \varphi) \left[ 1 + \frac {r \sin \varphi}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - r ^ {2} (1 - \cos \varphi) ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
1 - \cos \varphi = 2 \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{2} \varphi\right)
$$

$v_{A}$ 也可改写为

$$
v _ {A} = 2 v _ {0} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right) \left[ 1 + \frac {r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right)}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right)}} \right]
$$

取 B 为基点, 则 AB 板的运动学方程为

$$
x _ {B} = v _ {0} t - r \sin \varphi = v _ {0} t - r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right)
$$

$$
y _ {B} = r (1 - \cos \varphi) = r \left[ 1 - \cos \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right) \right]
$$

$$
\theta = \arcsin \left(\frac {y _ {B}}{l}\right) = \arcsin \left\{\frac {r}{l} \left[ 1 - \cos \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right) \right] \right\}
$$

5.1.13 求上题中 $AB$ 板的空间极迹和本体极迹方程(可用 $t$ 作为参数).

解 取上题所用的 $x, y$ 坐标，由于 $A$ 点的速度 $\pmb{v}_A$ 沿 $x$ 轴，可知瞬心 $C$ 的 $x$ 坐标

$$
x _ {C} = x _ {A} = x _ {B} - l \cos \theta = x _ {0} - r \sin \varphi - l \cos \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = v _ {0} t - r \sin \varphi - l \cos \theta \\ & = v _ {0} t - r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right) - \sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right)} \\ y _ {C} = \frac {v _ {A}}{\dot {\theta}} \end{array}
$$

上题已求得

$$
v _ {A} = 2 v _ {0} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right) \left[ 1 + \frac {r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right)}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right)}} \right]
$$

由

$$
\sin \theta = \frac {r}{l} (1 - \cos \varphi)
$$

$$
\cos \theta \cdot \dot {\theta} = \frac {r}{l} \sin \varphi \dot {\varphi} = \frac {v _ {0}}{l} \sin \varphi
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} = \frac {v _ {0} \sin \varphi}{l \cos \theta} = \frac {v _ {0} \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right)}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right)}}
$$

$$
y _ {C} = \frac {v _ {A}}{\dot {\theta}} = \tan \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right) \left[ r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right) + \sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right)} \right]
$$

上述 $x_{C}(t), y_{C}(t)$ 就是以 $t$ 为参数的空间极迹方程.

写本体极迹方程必须另取固连于 $AB$ 的坐标，今取 $Ax'y'$ ，原点在 $A$ 点， $x'$ 轴沿 $AB$ 方向， $y'$ 轴与之垂直，向上的方向为正，则本体极迹方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} x _ {C} ^ {\prime} = y _ {C} \sin \theta , \\ y _ {C} ^ {\prime} = y _ {C} \cos \theta . \end{array}
$$

将 $y_{C}$ 、 $\sin\theta$ 、 $\cos\theta$ 与t的关系代入上述两式即得以t为参数的本体极迹方程，

$$
x _ {C} ^ {\prime} = \frac {2 r}{\text { 乙 }} \tan \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right) \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right) \left[ r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{r} t\right) + \sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right)} \right]
$$

$$
y _ {C} ^ {\prime} = \frac {1}{l} \tan \left(\frac {v _ {0}}{2 r} t\right) \left[ l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {t}}{2 r} t\right) + r \sin \left(\frac {v _ {t}}{r} t\right) \sqrt {l ^ {2} - 4 r ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \left(\frac {v _ {t}}{2 r} t\right)} \right]
$$

5.1.14 证明均质的质量为 $m$ 的直棒的动能 $T = \frac{1}{6} m(u \cdot u + u \cdot v + v \cdot v)$ , 其中 $u, v$ 分别是棒两端的速度.

证明 方法一: 沿棒取 x 轴, x=0 处质元速度为 u, 设棒长为 l, x=l 端质元速度为 v.
x 处质元速度为

$$
u + \frac {v - u}{l} x
$$

按动能的定义，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {l} \left(u + \frac {v - u}{l} x\right) \cdot \left(u + \frac {v - u}{l} x\right) \frac {m}{l} \mathrm{d} x \\ & = \frac {m}{2 l} \int_ {0} ^ {l} \left[ u \cdot u + 2 u \cdot \frac {v - u}{l} x + (v \cdot v + u \cdot u - 2 v \cdot u) \frac {x ^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} \right] \mathrm{d} x \\ & = \frac {m}{2 l} \left[ u \cdot u l + l u \cdot (v - u) + \frac {1}{3} l (v \cdot v + u \cdot u - 2 v \cdot u) \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{6} m (u \cdot u + u \cdot v + v \cdot v). \end{array}
$$

方法二:用柯尼希定理,可不必积分.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {C} = \frac {1}{2} (\pmb {u} + \pmb {v})} \\ & {\qquad \pmb {v} = \pmb {u} + \omega \times \pmb {l}} \\ & {| \pmb {v} - \pmb {u} | = | \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {l} | = \omega l \sin \alpha} \end{array}
$$

其中 $\alpha$ 是 $\omega$ 与 l 的夹角，l 为速度为 v 的一端对速度为 u 的一端的位矢。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & I _ {C} = \frac {1}{1 2} m l ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha \\ & T = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {C} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {C} \omega^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m \left[ \frac {1}{2} (\boldsymbol {u} + \boldsymbol {v}) \right] ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} m l ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha \cdot \left(\frac {| \boldsymbol {v} - \boldsymbol {u} |}{l \sin \alpha}\right) ^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m \left[ \frac {1}{4} (\boldsymbol {u} \cdot \boldsymbol {u} + 2 \boldsymbol {u} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} + \boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}) + \frac {1}{1 2} (\boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} - 2 \boldsymbol {u} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} + \boldsymbol {u} \cdot \boldsymbol {u}) \right] \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{6} m (\boldsymbol {u} \cdot \boldsymbol {u} + \boldsymbol {u} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} + \boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}) \end{array}
$$

5.1.15 如图 5.17 曲柄 OA 以匀角速 $n_{0}=50r/min$ 绕定轴 O 转动，定齿轮的齿数为 $Z_{4}$ ，齿数 $Z_{1}, Z_{2}$ 的两齿轮紧固成一体装在曲柄 OA 上的 B 点。求装在 A 端的齿数 $Z_{3}$ 的齿轮转速。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/44694d2269ebc699fef888fa4578f9602ff49087b0294633a938faeaf69a0c52.jpg)



图5.17


解 取以 O 为原点随 OA 转动的参考系．规定 OA 的角速度的方向为正，齿轮为 $Z_{4}$ 的定齿轮的转速为 $-n_{0}$ ，其余三个齿轮的转速分别设为 $n_{1}^{\prime}, n_{2}^{\prime}, n_{3}^{\prime}$ ，由纯滚动条件

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (- n _ {0}) Z _ {4} & = - n _ {1} ^ {\prime} Z _ {1}, \quad n _ {1} ^ {\prime} = n _ {2} ^ {\prime} \\ n _ {2} ^ {\prime} Z _ {2} & = - n _ {3} ^ {\prime} Z _ {3} \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
n _ {3} ^ {\prime} = - \frac {Z _ {2}}{Z _ {3}} n _ {2} ^ {\prime} = - \frac {Z _ {2}}{Z _ {3}} n _ {1} ^ {\prime} = - \frac {Z _ {2}}{Z _ {3}} \cdot \frac {Z _ {4}}{Z _ {1}} n _ {0}
$$

相对于静参考系的转速为

$$
n _ {3} = n _ {0} + n _ {3} ^ {\prime} = n _ {0} \left(1 - \frac {Z _ {2} Z _ {4}}{Z _ {1} Z _ {3}}\right) = 5 0 \left(1 - \frac {Z _ {2} Z _ {4}}{Z _ {1} Z _ {3}}\right) (\mathrm{r/min})
$$

5.1.16 半径为 $b$ 的小圆柱在一个较大的半径为 $a$ 的圆柱内部纯滚动，如外面的圆柱围绕它的轴(固定轴)以角速度 $\Omega$ 转动，两圆柱的轴所在的平面以角速度 $\omega$ 绕固定轴转动，设 $\Omega, \omega$ 都是正的，求小圆柱的角速度。

解 考虑两圆柱相切的线的速度 v，

作为大圆柱上的点, $v=\Omega a$ .

作为小圆柱上的点,取小圆柱轴上的点为基点,考虑到小圆柱在大圆柱内做纯滚动.

$$
v = \omega (a - b) + \omega^ {\prime} b
$$

其中 $\omega'$ 是小圆柱的角速度, 其正方向的规定与 $\omega, \Omega$ 相同.

$$
\begin{array}{c} {\Omega a = \omega (a - b) + \omega^ {\prime} b} \\ {\omega^ {\prime} = \frac {1}{b} [ \Omega a - \omega (a - b) ] = \frac {1}{b} [ a (\Omega - \omega) + b \omega ]} \end{array}
$$

5.1.17 若做定点转动的刚体的运动学方程为

$$
\varphi = a t, \quad \psi = b t, \quad \theta = c
$$

式中 a、b、c 均为常量， $\varphi$ 、 $\psi$ 、 $\theta$ 为欧拉角、求角速度、角加速度在动、静坐标系中的分量。

解

$$
\dot {\varphi} = a, \quad \dot {\psi} = b, \quad \dot {\theta} = 0.
$$

角速度 $\omega$ 、角加速度 $\beta$ 在固连于刚体的动坐标系 $x, y, z$ 轴上的分量计算如下：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega_ {x} = \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \sin \psi + \dot {\theta} \cos \psi = a \mathrm{sinc} \sin b t} \\ & {\omega_ {y} = \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \cos \psi - \dot {\theta} \sin \psi = a \mathrm{sinc} \cos b t} \\ & {\omega_ {z} = \dot {\varphi} \cos \theta + \dot {\psi} = b + a \mathrm{cos} c} \\ & {\omega = \omega_ {x} i + \omega_ {y} j + \omega_ {z} k} \\ & {\beta = \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} + \omega \times \omega = \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t}} \\ & {\quad = \dot {\omega} _ {x} i + \dot {\omega} _ {y} j + \dot {\omega} _ {z} k} \\ & {\beta_ {x} = \dot {\omega} _ {x} = a b \mathrm{sinc} \cos b t} \\ & {\beta_ {y} = \dot {\omega} _ {y} = - a b \mathrm{sinc} \sin b t} \\ & {\beta_ {z} = \dot {\omega} _ {z} = 0} \end{array}
$$

角速度 $\omega$ 、角加速度 $\beta$ 在静坐标系 $\xi$ 、 $\eta$ 、 $\zeta$ 轴上的分量计算如下：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega_ {\xi} = \dot {\theta} \cos \varphi + \dot {\psi} \sin \theta \sin \varphi = b \mathrm{sincsinat} \\ \omega_ {\eta} = \dot {\theta} \sin \varphi - \dot {\psi} \sin \theta \cos \varphi = - b \mathrm{sinccosat} \\ \omega_ {\zeta} = \dot {\varphi} + \dot {\psi} \cos \theta = a + b \mathrm{cosc} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \beta_ {\xi} = \dot {\omega} _ {\xi} = a b \mathrm{sinccosat} \\ \beta_ {\eta} = \dot {\omega} _ {\eta} = a b \mathrm{sincsinat} \\ \beta_ {\zeta} = \dot {\omega} _ {\zeta} = 0 \end{array}
$$

5.1.18 刚体做上题的运动, 求刚体上 $(1,0,0)$ 点及位于空间 $(1,0,0)$ 的刚体上的点在 $t$ 时刻的速度和加速度.

解 方法一: t 时刻刚体上 $(1,0,0)$ 点 (即 x=1, y=0, z=0 的点) 的速度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {v} & = \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {r} = (\omega_ {x} \boldsymbol {i} + \omega_ {y} \boldsymbol {j} + \omega_ {z} \boldsymbol {k}) \times \boldsymbol {i} = \omega_ {z} \boldsymbol {j} - \omega_ {y} \boldsymbol {k} \\ & = (b + a \cos c) \boldsymbol {j} - a \sin c \cos b t \boldsymbol {k} \end{array}
$$

该点的加速度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {a} = \frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {v}}{\mathrm{d} t} = (b + a \cos c) \frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {j}}{\mathrm{d} t} + a b \sin c \sin b t \pmb {k} - a \sin c - \cos b t \frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {k}}{\mathrm{d} t}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {j}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {j} = (\omega_ {x} \pmb {i} + \omega_ {y} \pmb {j} + \omega_ {z} \pmb {k}) \times \pmb {j}} \\ & {\qquad = - \omega_ {z} \pmb {i} + \omega_ {x} \pmb {k} = - (b + a \cos c) \pmb {i} + a \sin c \sin b t \pmb {k}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {k}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {k} = (\omega_ {x} \pmb {i} + \omega_ {y} \pmb {j} + \omega_ {z} \pmb {k}) \times \pmb {k}} \\ & {\qquad = \omega_ {y} \pmb {i} - \omega_ {x} \pmb {j} = a \sin c \cos b t \pmb {i} - a \sin c \sin b t \pmb {j}} \end{array}
$$

将 $\frac{\mathrm{d}j}{\mathrm{d}t}, \frac{\mathrm{d}k}{\mathrm{d}t}$ 代入 $a$ 的式子，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} = & - \left[ (b + a \cos c) ^ {2} + (a \sin c \cos b t) ^ {2} \right] \boldsymbol {i} \\ & + (a \sin c) ^ {2} \sin b t \cos b t \boldsymbol {j} + a \sin c (2 b + a \cos c) \sin b t \boldsymbol {k} \end{array}
$$

方法二：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {u} & = \boldsymbol {\omega} \times (\boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {r}) + \boldsymbol {\beta} \times \boldsymbol {r} \\ & = \boldsymbol {\omega} (\boldsymbol {\omega} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}) - \boldsymbol {r} \omega^ {2} + \boldsymbol {\beta} \times \boldsymbol {r} \end{array}
$$

代入 r=i,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} & = \omega_ {x} \boldsymbol {\omega} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {i} + \beta_ {z} \boldsymbol {j} - \beta_ {y} \boldsymbol {k} \\ & = a \sin c \sin b t [ a \sin c \sin b t \boldsymbol {i} + a \sin c \cos b t \boldsymbol {j} + (b + a \cos c) \boldsymbol {k} ] \\ & - [ a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} c + (b + a \cos c) ^ {2} ] \boldsymbol {i} + a b \sin c \sin b t \boldsymbol {k} \end{array}
$$

以下计算略,可得与方法一同样的结果.

t 时刻位于空间 $(1,0,0)$ 即 $\xi=1,\eta=0,\zeta=0$ 处刚体上的点的速度和加速度分别计算如下：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {v} & = \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {r} = (\omega_ {\xi} \boldsymbol {\xi} ^ {\circ} + \omega_ {\eta} \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\circ} + \omega_ {\zeta} \boldsymbol {\zeta}) ^ {\circ} \times \boldsymbol {\xi} ^ {\circ} = \omega_ {\zeta} \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\circ} - \omega_ {\eta} \boldsymbol {\zeta} ^ {\circ} \\ & = (a + b \cos c) \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\circ} + b \sin c \cos a t \boldsymbol {\zeta} ^ {\circ} \\ a & = \boldsymbol {\omega} \times (\boldsymbol {\omega} \times \boldsymbol {r}) + \boldsymbol {\beta} \times \boldsymbol {r} \\ & = \boldsymbol {\omega} (\boldsymbol {\omega} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}) - \boldsymbol {r} \omega^ {2} + \boldsymbol {\beta} \times \boldsymbol {r} \end{array}
$$

代入 $r = \xi^{\circ}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} & = \omega_ {\xi} \boldsymbol {\omega} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {\xi} ^ {\circ} + \beta_ {\zeta} \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\circ} - \beta_ {\eta} \boldsymbol {\zeta} ^ {\circ} \\ & = b \sin c \sin a t [ b \sin c \sin a t \boldsymbol {\xi} ^ {\circ} - b \sin c \cos a t \boldsymbol {\eta} ^ {\circ} ] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & + (a + b \cos c) \zeta^ {\circ} ] - [ (b \sin c) ^ {2} + (a + b \cos c) ^ {2} ] \zeta^ {\circ} \\ & - a b \sin c \sin a t \zeta^ {\circ} \\ = & - [ (a + b \cos c) ^ {2} + (b \sin c \cos a t) ^ {2} ] \xi^ {\circ} \\ & - (b \sin c) ^ {2} \sin a t \cos a t \eta^ {\circ} + b ^ {2} \sin c \cos c \sin a t \zeta^ {\circ} \end{array}
$$

注意:不能用前面算出的v用 $a=\frac{d\quad v}{dt}$ 求a. 这样计算的 $\frac{d\quad v}{dt}$ 不是位于空间那点在t时刻的加速度,而是位于空间那点的刚体上的点的速度变化率.

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {v}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \lim _ {\Delta t \rightarrow v} \frac {\boldsymbol {v} (t + \Delta t) - \boldsymbol {v} (t)}{\Delta t}
$$

$t$ 和 $t + \Delta t$ 位于的点是刚体上不同的点，因此这个速度的时间变化率不是处于那点的刚体上的点的加速度.

5.1.19 刚体做 5.1.17 题所给的定点转动, 求本体极面和空间极面满足的方程.

解 本体极面和空间极面满足的方程是

$$
\omega \times r = 0
$$

本体极面方程代入 $\omega = \omega_{x}\mathbf{i} + \omega_{y}\mathbf{j} + \omega_{z}\mathbf{k},\mathbf{r} = x\mathbf{i} + y\mathbf{j} + z\mathbf{k}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c c} i & j & k \\ a \sin c \sin b t & a \sin c \cos b t & b + a \cos c \\ x & y & z \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {\quad a y \sin c \sin b t - a x \sin c \cos b t = 0} \\ & {\quad a z \sin c \sin b t - (b + a \cos c) x = 0} \\ & {\quad a z \sin c \cos b t - (b + a \cos c) y = 0} \\ & {\quad \frac {x}{z} = \frac {a \sin c \sin b t}{b + a \cos c}} \\ & {\quad \frac {y}{z} = \frac {a \sin c \cos b t}{b + a \cos c}} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

(2) 

式(1)、(2)两式平方相加,可消去 t 得不含参数的本体极面方程:

$$
x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} = \frac {a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} c}{(b + a \cos c) ^ {2}} z ^ {2},
$$

求空间极面方程，用 $\omega = \omega_{\xi}\xi^{\circ} + \omega_{\eta}\eta^{\circ} + \omega_{\zeta}\zeta^{\circ}, r = \xi \xi^{\circ} + \eta \eta^{\circ} + \zeta \zeta^{\circ}$ ，代入 $\omega \times r = 0$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega_ {\eta} \zeta - \omega_ {\zeta} \eta = 0 \\ \omega_ {\xi} \zeta - \omega_ {\xi} \xi = 0 \end{array}
$$

即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (- b \sin c \cos a t) \zeta - (a + b \cos c) \eta & = 0 \\ (b \sin c \sin a t) \zeta - (a + b \cos c) \xi & = 0 \\ \frac {\eta}{\zeta} & = \frac {- b \sin c \cos a t}{a + b \cos c} \\ \frac {\xi}{\zeta} & = \frac {b \sin c \sin a t}{a + b \cos c} \end{array}
$$

消去 $t$ 得不含参数的空间极面方程

$$
\xi^ {2} + \eta^ {2} = \frac {b ^ {2} \sin^ {2} c}{(a + b \cos c) ^ {2}} \zeta^ {2}
$$

5.1.20 开始固连于刚体的动坐标系 $Oxyz$ 与静坐标系 $O\xi \eta \zeta$ 完全重合 $(x, y, z$ 轴分别与 $\xi, \eta, \zeta$ 轴重合). 动系先绕 $\xi$ 轴转 $\theta$ 角, 再绕新的 $y$ 轴转 $\psi$ 角, 最后绕新的 $z$ 轴转 $\varphi$ 角. 刚体的位置用此 $\theta, \psi, \varphi$ 来描述. 像推导欧拉运动学方程那样, 分别写出做定点转动的刚体的角速度在固连于刚体的 $xyz$ 坐标系的三个分量, 与在 $\xi \eta \zeta$ 静坐标系的三个分量, 与这里定义的 $\theta, \psi, \varphi$ 三个角及其对时间的导数的关系.

解 动坐标系三次转动的过程如图 5.18 所示．开始，Oxyz 与 Oξηζ 完全重合，第一次转动绕 ξ 轴转 θ 角，x 轴（图中 x' 轴）不动，y、z 轴分别转到图中 y'、z' 轴的位置；第二次转动绕新的 y 轴（即图中 y' 轴）转 ψ 角，z' 轴转到最后位置（图中 z 轴），x' 轴转到 x'', y' 不动．第三次转动绕图中 z 轴转 φ 角，x''、y' 分别转到 x、y 轴的最后位置（图中 x、y 轴）．共面的轴用光滑的虚线相连．z'、ζ、y'、η 四轴共面，z'、z、ξ、(x)、x''轴共面，x''、x、y'、y 轴共面，ON 是 xy 平面与 ηζ 平面的交线，ON 也就是 y' 轴．

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/4f5a41137b564c205f2f98ed7ad750bc15c42e0beb6894592a125396647b10bf.jpg)



图5.18


$\theta$ 是绕 $\xi$ 轴转动的, $\psi$ 是绕 $y'$ 轴即 ON 转动的, $\psi$ 是绕 z 轴转动的, 故

$$
\omega = \dot {\theta} \xi^ {\circ} + \dot {\psi} n ^ {\circ} + \dot {\varphi} k
$$

其中 $n^{\circ}$ 是 ON 轴的单位矢量.

将 $\xi^{\circ}$ 、 $n^{\circ}$ 用i、j、k表示，就可得到角速度在动坐标系中的三个分量：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {\xi} ^ {\circ} = \cos \psi \pmb {i} ^ {\prime \prime} + \sin \psi \pmb {k} = \cos \psi \Big [ \cos \varphi   \pmb {i} + \cos \Big (\varphi + \frac {\pi}{2} \Big)   \pmb {j} \Big ] + \sin \psi \pmb {k}} \\ & {\qquad = \cos \psi \cos \varphi   \pmb {i} - \cos \psi \sin \varphi   \pmb {j} + \sin \psi \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {n} ^ {\circ} = \sin \varphi   \pmb {i} + \cos \varphi   \pmb {j}} \\ & {\qquad \omega = \dot {\theta} (\cos \psi \cos \varphi   \pmb {i} - \cos \psi \sin \varphi   \pmb {j} + \sin \psi   \pmb {k}) + \dot {\psi} (\sin \varphi   \pmb {i} + \cos \varphi   \pmb {j}) + \dot {\varphi}   \pmb {k}} \\ & {\qquad = \omega_ {x}   \pmb {i} + \omega_ {y}   \pmb {j} + \omega_ {z}   \pmb {k}} \\ & {\omega_ {x} = \dot {\psi} \sin \varphi + \dot {\theta} \cos \psi \cos \varphi} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega_ {y} = \dot {\psi} \cos \varphi - \dot {\theta} \cos \psi \sin \varphi \\ \omega_ {z} = \dot {\varphi} + \dot {\theta} \sin \psi \end{array}
$$

将 $n^{\circ}, k$ 用 $\xi^{\circ}, \eta^{\circ}, \zeta^{\circ}$ 表示，可得到角速度在静坐标系中的三个分量：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {n ^ {\circ} = \cos \theta \eta^ {\circ} + \sin \theta \zeta^ {\circ}} \\ & {k = \cos \psi k ^ {\prime} + \sin \psi \xi^ {\circ}} \\ & {\qquad = \cos \psi \Big [ \cos \theta   \zeta^ {\circ} + \cos \Big (\theta + \frac {\pi}{2} \Big) \eta^ {\circ} \Big ] + \sin \psi \xi^ {\circ}} \\ & {\qquad = \sin \psi \xi^ {\circ} - \cos \psi \sin \theta \eta^ {\circ} + \cos \psi \cos \theta \zeta^ {\circ}} \\ & {\omega = \dot {\theta}   \xi^ {\circ} + \dot {\psi}   (\cos \theta \eta^ {\circ} + \sin \theta \zeta^ {\circ}) + \dot {\varphi}   (\sin \psi \xi^ {\circ}} \\ & {\qquad - \cos \psi \sin \theta \eta^ {\circ} + \cos \psi \cos \theta \zeta^ {\circ})} \\ & {\qquad = \omega_ {\xi} \xi^ {\circ} + \omega_ {\eta} \eta^ {\circ} + \omega_ {\zeta} \zeta^ {\circ}} \\ & {\omega_ {\xi} = \dot {\theta} + \dot {\varphi} \sin \psi} \\ & {\omega_ {\eta} = \dot {\psi}   \cos \theta - \dot {\varphi} \cos \psi \sin \theta} \\ & {\omega_ {\zeta} = \dot {\psi}   \sin \theta + \dot {\varphi} \cos \psi \cos \theta} \end{array}
$$

## 5.2 刚体的相对运动

5.2.1 一个半径为 $a$ 、圆心为 $A$ 的圆柱在一个水平面上做无滑滚动，圆心 $A$ 以恒定速度 $u$ 运动；另一半径为 $b$ 、圆心为 $B$ 的第二个圆柱（它的轴与第一个圆柱的轴平行）在第一个圆柱上做无滑滚动， $B$ 点相对于固连于第一个圆柱的坐标系以恒定的速率 $v$ 运动。 $P$ 点是第二个圆柱边缘上的点，若 $t = 0$ 时， $A, B, P$ 在同一铅垂线上。求 $P$ 点的速度、加速度和时间的关系。

解 $AB$ 连线相对于固连于第一个圆柱的参考系的角速度为

$$
\omega_ {A B} ^ {\prime} = \frac {v}{a + b}
$$

第一个圆柱对随 $A$ 平动的参考系或对静参考系的角速度为

$$
\omega_ {A} = \frac {u}{a}
$$

AB 连线相对于随 A 平动的参考系或对静参考系的角速度为

$$
\omega_ {A B} = \dot {\alpha} = \omega_ {A} + \omega_ {A B} ^ {\prime} = \frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}
$$

其中 $\alpha$ 是 AB 线与竖直线(向上方向)的夹角.

设第二个圆柱对 A 平动参考系或对静参考系的角速度为 $\omega_{\beta}$ 或 $\dot{\beta}, \beta$ 是 BP 线与竖直线(向上方向)的夹角(设 P 点在 t=0 时位于第二个圆柱的最高点).

以上对 $\omega_{AB}^{\prime}, \omega_{B}$ 的正向规定都是与 $\omega_{A}$ 的正向相同.

考虑到两圆柱体间的相对运动是纯滚动,两圆柱的两接触点对任何参考系都有相同的速度,对随 A 点平动的参考系,有

$$
\omega_ {A B} (a + b) - \omega_ {B} \cdot b = \omega_ {A} a
$$

即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) (a + b) - \omega_ {B} b = \frac {u}{a} \cdot a} \\ & {\dot {\beta} = \omega_ {B} = \frac {1}{b} \left(\frac {u}{a} b + v\right) = \frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}} \end{array}
$$

对静参考系, P 点的速度可表为

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {P} = \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {P} ^ {\prime} = \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {A B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {A B} + \boldsymbol {\omega} _ {B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {P} ^ {\prime}\tag{1}
$$

其中 $r_{P}^{\prime}$ 是 P 点对 B 点的位矢, $r_{AB}$ 是 B 点对 A 点的位矢(两圆柱都做平面平行运动, 取共面的代表平面, A、B、P 都是代表平面上的点).

取 i 为水平方向(沿 u 的方向)的单位矢量, j 是竖直向下的单位矢量, 则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & v A = u i, \omega_ {A B} = \dot {\alpha} k, \omega_ {B} = \dot {\beta} k \\ & r _ {A B} = (a + b) (\sin \alpha i - \cos \alpha j) \\ & r _ {P} ^ {\prime} = b (\sin \beta i - \cos \beta j) \end{array}
$$

将这些式子代入式(1)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {P} = u \pmb {i} + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) \pmb {k} \times (a + b) (\sin \alpha \pmb {i} - \cos \alpha \pmb {j})} \\ & {\qquad + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}\right) \pmb {k} \times b (\sin \beta \pmb {i} - \cos \beta \pmb {j})} \\ & {\qquad = \left[ u + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) (a + b) \cos \alpha + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}\right) b \cos \beta \right] \pmb {i}} \\ & {\qquad + \left[ \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) (a + b) \sin \alpha + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}\right) b \sin \beta \right] \pmb {j}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P} = \frac {\mathrm{d}   \pmb {v} _ {P}}{\mathrm{d} t} = - \left[ \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) ^ {2} (a + b) \sin \alpha + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}\right) ^ {2} b \sin \beta \right] \pmb {i}} \\ & {\qquad + \left[ \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) ^ {2} (a + b) \cos \alpha + \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}\right) ^ {2} b \cos \beta \right] \pmb {j}} \\ & {\qquad \alpha = \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{a + b}\right) t, \quad \beta = \left(\frac {u}{a} + \frac {v}{b}\right) t} \end{array}
$$

其中

计算 $P$ 点的加速度也可用下列公式：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} _ {P} & = \boldsymbol {a} _ {A} + \dot {\omega} _ {A B} \times (\omega_ {A B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {A B}) + \omega_ {A B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {A B} \\ & + \omega_ {B} \times (\omega_ {B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {P} ^ {\prime}) + \dot {\omega} _ {B} \times \boldsymbol {r} _ {P} ^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

其中 $a_{A}$ 、 $\dot{\omega}_{AB}$ 、 $\dot{\omega}_{B}$ 在这里均为零。

显然比上述直接求导的方法麻烦.

5.2.2 A、B、C 三个半径均为 r 的圆盘，紧挨着安装在一曲柄上．除 A 盘不动外，B、C 两盘可分别绕 $O_{2}$ 、 $O_{3}$ 转动，曲柄围绕 $O_{1}$ 转动．整个机构在同一平面上．B、C 盘均做滚动．若 B 盘的转动角速度为 $\omega(t)$ ．求：

(1) C 盘的转动角速度；

(2) t 时刻位于 B、C 两盘相接触的两点 P、Q 的加速度的大小；

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/6553ac8293bbc8a0bc3b594662fc0d1673dae77f0a91b22af1bb44e6ed8d5a3c.jpg)



图5.19


(3) 若 t=0 时, P 点在图示位置, 求任何时刻 P 点的加速度的大小.
解 （1）规定垂直纸面向下的角速度为正值，设曲柄的角速度为 $\Omega$ .
考虑 B 盘上与 A 盘相接触的点的速度为零，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 2 r \Omega - r \omega (t) & = 0 \\ \Omega & = \frac {1}{2} \omega (t) \end{array}
$$

设 C 盘的角速度为 $\omega_{C}$ ，考虑 P、Q 两点速度相等，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 4 r \Omega - r \omega_ {C} & = 2 r \Omega + r \omega \\ \omega_ {C} & = 2 \Omega - \omega = 0 \end{array}
$$

(2) 求 P 点的绝对加速度 $a_{P}$ ，动系用 $O_{2}$ 平动参考系，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {a _ {P e} = 2 r \Omega^ {2} n + 2 r \dot {\Omega} \tau} \\ & {\qquad = 2 r \left(\frac {\omega}{2}\right) ^ {2} n + 2 r \cdot \frac {1}{2} \dot {\omega} \tau = \frac {1}{2} r \omega^ {2} n + r \dot {\omega} \tau} \\ & {a _ {P r} = r \omega^ {2} n + r \dot {\omega} \tau} \\ & {a _ {P} = a _ {P e} + a _ {P r} = \frac {3}{2} r \omega^ {2} n + 2 r \dot {\omega} \tau} \\ & {a _ {P} = \sqrt {a _ {P n} ^ {2} + a _ {P \tau} ^ {2}} = \frac {r}{2} \sqrt {9 \omega^ {4} + 1 6 \dot {\omega} ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

求 Q 点的绝对加速度 $a_{Q}$ ，动系用 $O_{3}$ 平动参考系，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} a _ {Q e} & = 4 r \Omega^ {2} n + 4 r \dot {\Omega} \tau = r \omega^ {2} n + 2 r \dot {\omega} \tau \\ a _ {Q r} & = r \omega_ {C} ^ {2} n ^ {\prime} + r \dot {\omega} _ {C} \tau^ {\prime} = 0 \\ a _ {Q} & = a _ {Q e} + a _ {Q r} = r \omega^ {2} n + 2 r \dot {\omega} \tau \\ a _ {Q} & = \sqrt {(r \omega^ {2}) ^ {2} + (2 r \dot {\omega}) ^ {2}} = r \sqrt {\omega^ {4} + 4 \dot {\omega} ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

(3) 取固定坐标 $xy$ , 原点在 $O_1, x$ 轴沿图示位置 $(t = 0$ 时) $O_1P$ 的方向, $y$ 轴向右下方为正方向. 取 $O_2$ 平动参考系为动系,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {a _ {e} = \frac {1}{2} r \omega^ {2} n + r \dot {\omega} \tau} \\ & {n = - \cos \int_ {0} ^ {t} \Omega (t) \mathrm{d} t i - \sin \int_ {0} ^ {t} \Omega (t) \mathrm{d} t j} \\ & {\quad = - \cos \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t i - \sin \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t j} \\ & {\tau = - \sin \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t i + \cos \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t j} \end{array}
$$

为书写简便起见，令

$$
\alpha = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t
$$

它是曲柄在 $0 \sim t$ 期间的角位移.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} _ {e} & = - \left(\frac {1}{2} r \omega^ {2} \cos \alpha + r \dot {\omega} \sin \alpha\right) \boldsymbol {i} + \left(- \frac {1}{2} r \omega^ {2} \sin \alpha + r \dot {\omega} \cos \alpha\right) \boldsymbol {j} \\ & \quad \boldsymbol {a} _ {r} = r \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {n} ^ {\prime} + r \dot {\omega} \boldsymbol {\tau} ^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

注意这里的法向、切向单位矢量与 $a_{e}$ 式子中的法向、切向单位矢量方向不一致，必须加以区别，而在（2）问中计算 $a_{Pe}, a_{Pr}$ 中的两个 $\pmb{n}, \tau$ 是一致的， $a_{Qe}, a_{Qr}$ 中的不一致也加以区别了，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & n ^ {\prime} = - \cos \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t i - \sin \int_ {0} ^ {t} \omega (t) \mathrm{d} t j \\ & \quad = - \cos 2 \alpha i - \sin 2 \alpha j \\ & \tau^ {\prime} = - \sin 2 \alpha i + \cos 2 \alpha j \\ & a _ {r} = - (r \omega^ {2} \cos 2 \alpha + r \dot {\omega} \sin 2 \alpha) i + (- r \omega^ {2} \sin 2 \alpha + r \dot {\omega} \cos 2 \alpha) j \\ & a _ {P} = \sqrt {(a _ {e x} + a _ {r x}) ^ {2} + (a _ {e y} + a _ {r y}) ^ {2}} \\ & \quad = \left[ \left(- \frac {1}{2} r \omega^ {2} \cos \alpha - r \dot {\omega} \sin \alpha - r \omega^ {2} \cos 2 \alpha - r \dot {\omega} \sin 2 \alpha\right) ^ {2} + \left(- \frac {1}{2} r \omega^ {2} \sin \alpha + r \dot {\omega} \cos \alpha - r \omega^ {2} \sin 2 \alpha + r \dot {\omega} \cos 2 \alpha\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ & \quad = \left[ \frac {1}{4} r ^ {2} \omega^ {4} + r ^ {2} \dot {\omega} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \omega^ {4} + r ^ {2} \dot {\omega} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \omega^ {4} \cos \alpha + r ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \dot {\omega} \sin \alpha - 2 r ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \dot {\omega} \sin \alpha + 2 r ^ {2} \dot {\omega} ^ {2} \cos \alpha \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ & = r \left[ \frac {5}{4} \omega^ {4} + 2 \dot {\omega} ^ {2} + \omega^ {4} \cos \alpha - \omega^ {2} \dot {\omega} \sin \alpha + 2 \dot {\omega} ^ {2} \cos \alpha \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ & = r [ \omega^ {4} (\frac {5}{4} + \cos \alpha) - \omega^ {2} \dot {\omega} \sin \alpha + 2 \dot {\omega} ^ {2} (1 + \cos \alpha) ] ^ {\frac {1}{2}}. \end{array}
$$

当 $\alpha=2n\pi(n=0,1,2,\cdots)$ 时，P 点与 C 盘相切， $a_{P}$ 值与(2)问的结果相同。

注意:(2)问中的 P 点是 B 盘与 C 盘相切的点,(3)问中的 P 点不是时时与 C 盘相切的, $a_{P}$ 是 t 的函数,而在 $\alpha=2n\pi$ 那些时刻, $a_{P}$ 值都一样,都在图示的与 C 盘相切的位置.

5.2.3 一半径为 $a$ 的圆盘 $A$ 以等角速 $\omega_{A}$ 绕其固定中心 $O$ 转动，另一半径为 $b$ 的圆

盘 B 以等角速 $\omega_{B}$ 绕其中心 $O'$ 在前一圆盘外侧纯滚动(如图 5.20 所示). 求:

(1) 两盘的连杆 $OO'$ 的转动角速度 $\Omega$ ;

(2) B 盘在 A 盘上转动回到 A 盘上同一接触点所需的最短时间 $t_{1}$ ;

(3) B 盘上与 A 盘相接触的点又回到与 A 盘相接触的位置所需的最短时间 $t_{2}$ ;

(4) $O'$ 点回到空间原来位置所需的最短时间 $t_3$ ;

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/be9e5b6f36206152c6fbdbc280bf71b9d084ec712d982bfeb85d36567a13c02d.jpg)


（5）如 $a = 5\mathrm{cm}, b = 3\mathrm{cm}, \omega_A = 20\pi \mathrm{s}^{-1}, \omega_B = 36\pi \mathrm{s}^{-1}, B$ 盘所有点回到空间原来位置所需的最小时间 $t_4, A, B$ 盘所有点回到空间原来位置所需的最小时间 $t_5, A$ 盘所有点及 $O'$ 点回到空间原来位置所需的最小时间 $t_6$ .

图5.20

解 （1）设连杆 $OO'$ 的角速度为 $\Omega$ ，逆时针转动的角速度为正。

由纯滚动条件，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Omega_ {A} a & = \Omega (a + b) - \omega_ {B} b \\ \Omega & = \frac {\omega_ {A} a + \omega_ {B} b}{a + b} \end{array}
$$

(2) $B$ 盘在 $A$ 盘上转动回到 $A$ 盘上的同一点相切所需的最短时间, 是连杆 $OO'$ 相对于 $A$ 转一周的时间.

连杆 $OO'$ 相对于 A 盘的角速度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Omega - \omega_ {A} & = \frac {\omega_ {A} a + \omega_ {B} b}{a + b} - \omega_ {A} = \frac {(\omega_ {B} - \omega_ {A}) b}{a + b} \\ t _ {1} & = \frac {2 \pi}{| \Omega - \omega_ {A} |} = \frac {2 \pi (a + b)}{b | \omega_ {B} - \omega_ {A} |} \end{array}
$$

(3) $B$ 盘上与 $A$ 盘相接触的点又回到与 $A$ 盘相接触, 并不要求在图示的同一位置, 也不要求与 $A$ 盘在先前的同一点相接触, 所要求的时间是 $B$ 盘相对于连杆 $OO'$ 转一周所需的时间.

B 盘相对于连杆 $OO'$ 的角速度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \omega_ {B} - \Omega & = \omega_ {B} - \frac {\omega_ {A} a + \omega_ {B} b}{a + b} = \frac {(\omega_ {B} - \omega_ {A}) a}{a + b} \\ t _ {2} & = \frac {2 \pi}{| \omega_ {B} - \Omega |} = \frac {2 \pi (a + b)}{a | \omega_ {B} - \omega_ {A} |} \end{array}
$$

(4) $O'$ 点回到空间原来位置所需的最短时间就是连杆 $OO'$ 的周期，

$$
t _ {3} = \frac {2 \pi}{\Omega} = \frac {2 \pi (a + b)}{\omega_ {A} a + \omega_ {B} b}
$$

(5) 今 $a = 5\mathrm{cm}, b = 3\mathrm{cm}, \omega_A = 20\pi \mathrm{s}^{-1}, \omega_B = 36\pi \mathrm{s}^{-1}$ ,

$$
\Omega = \frac {\omega_ {A} a + \omega_ {B} b}{a + b} = \frac {2 0 \pi \times 5 + 3 6 \pi \times 3}{5 + 3} = 2 6 \pi \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}
$$

$t=t_{4}$ 时，B 盘所有点回到空间原来位置， $\omega_{B}t_{4}$ 必为 $2\pi$ 的整数倍，此时连杆 $OO'$ 也回到空间原来位置，故 $\Omega t_{4}$ 也必为 $2\pi$ 的整数倍，求最短时间，两个整数之比必须是不可约的有理数，

$$
\frac {\omega_ {B} t _ {4}}{2 \pi} \Big / \frac {\Omega t _ {4}}{2 \pi} = \frac {\omega_ {B}}{\Omega} = \frac {3 6 \pi}{2 6 \pi} = \frac {1 8}{1 3}
$$

$13\omega_{B} = 18\Omega$ ，或 $\omega_{B}:\Omega = 18:13$ 

$$
t _ {4} = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega_ {B}} \times 1 8 \text {或} \frac {2 \pi}{\Omega} \times 1 3 = 1 \mathrm{s}
$$

$\left(t_{4}\text{是}\frac{2\pi}{\Omega}\text{及}\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{B}}\right.$ 的不可约的整数倍．

$t_{5}$ 是 A、B 盘的所有点回到空间原来位置所需的最小时间,也是 A、B 盘及 $O'$ 点回到空间原来位置所需的最小时间,照上述的考虑:

$$
\omega_ {A}: \omega_ {B}: \Omega = 1 0: 1 8: 1 3
$$

$$
t _ {5} = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega_ {A}} \times 1 0 = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega_ {B}} \times 1 8 = \frac {2 \pi}{\Omega} \times 1 3 = 1 \mathrm{s}
$$

同样考虑 A 盘所有点及 $O'$ 点回到空间原来位置所需的最小时间 $t_{6}$ ，

$$
\omega_ {A}: \Omega = 1 0: 1 3
$$

$$
t _ {6} = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega_ {A}} \times 1 0 = \frac {2 \pi}{\Omega} \times 1 3 = 1 \mathrm{s}
$$

5.2.4 高为 $h$ 、顶角为 $2\alpha$ 的正圆锥在一水平面上绕顶点作纯滚动，若已知其几何对称轴以恒定的角速度 $\Omega$ 绕竖直轴转动．求此刻圆锥底面上最高点 $A$ 的速度和加速度．

解 用 xyz 动坐标系来表达, x 轴始终是圆锥与水平面的交线, 如图 5.21 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/14e920f68303ea182c02daea22a2437c582f7ebee07175d2e55fe3f42fae9128.jpg)



图5.21


方法一:用刚体上一点的速度和加速度公式.

设刚体的角速度的大小为 $\omega$ ，

$$
\omega = - \omega i
$$

底面中心 $D$ 的速度为

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {D} = \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \overrightarrow {O D} = - \omega \boldsymbol {i} \times (h \cos \alpha \boldsymbol {i} + h \sin \alpha \boldsymbol {k}) = \omega h \sin \alpha \boldsymbol {j}
$$

又知 $OD$ 的角速度为 $\Omega k$ 

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {D} = \Omega \boldsymbol {k} \times (h \cos \alpha \boldsymbol {i} + h \sin \alpha \boldsymbol {k}) = \Omega h \cos \alpha \boldsymbol {j}
$$

$$
\therefore \omega h \sin \alpha = \Omega h \cos \alpha , \quad \omega = \Omega \cot \alpha
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \omega & = - \Omega \cot \alpha i \\ \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} & = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (- \Omega \cot \alpha i) = - \Omega \cot \alpha \frac {\mathrm{d} i}{\mathrm{d} t} \\ & = - \Omega \cot \alpha \Omega k \times i = - \Omega^ {2} \cot \alpha j \\ v _ {A} & = \omega \times \overrightarrow {O A} = - \Omega \cot \alpha i \times h \sec \alpha (\cos 2 \alpha_ {i} + \sin 2 \alpha k) \\ & = \Omega h \cot \alpha \sec \alpha \sin 2 \alpha j = 2 \Omega h \cos \alpha j \\ a _ {A} & = \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \overrightarrow {O A} + \omega \times (\omega \times \overrightarrow {O A}) \\ & = - \Omega^ {2} \cot \alpha j \times h \sec \alpha (\cos 2 \alpha i + \sin 2 \alpha k) - \Omega \cot \alpha i \times 2 \Omega h \cos \alpha j \\ & = - 2 \Omega^ {2} h \cos \alpha i - \Omega^ {2} h \csc \alpha k \end{array}
$$

方法二:由角速度合成公式求刚体的角速度 $\omega$ ,以下同方法一.

刚体的定点转动, 取其几何对称轴为固连于刚体的动坐标系的 $z$ 轴, 静坐标系的 $\zeta$ 轴竖直向上, 则刚体做规则进动. 图 5.22 中的 $\omega'$ 是自转角速度, $\Omega$ 是进动角速度, 章动角速度等于零, $\omega$ 是刚体的角速度.

$$
\omega = \Omega + \omega^ {\prime}, \quad \omega = \Omega \cot \alpha
$$

一般的教科书在刚体运动学中不讲规则进动，也可将图中的 $\Omega$ 、 $\omega'$ 分别视为图中动系 $Oxyz$ 的角速度和刚体对动参考系的角速度，从而得出 $\omega = \Omega + \omega'$ 和 $\omega = \Omega \cot \alpha$ .

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/8399ec78371d2505139bbba37b2f205c1692a98d6fb36e2ac2b0600427115bb4.jpg)


方法三: 将 Oxyz 作为动参考系, 用相对运动的知识.


图5.22


$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} & = \boldsymbol {v} _ {A r} + \boldsymbol {v} _ {A e} = \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {\prime} \times \overrightarrow {O A} + \boldsymbol {\Omega} \times \overrightarrow {O A} \\ & \quad \boldsymbol {a} _ {A} = \boldsymbol {a} _ {A r} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {A e} + \boldsymbol {a} _ {A c} ^ {\prime} \\ & \quad \boldsymbol {a} _ {A r} = \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {\prime} \times (\boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {\prime} \times \overrightarrow {O A}) = \boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {\prime} \times \boldsymbol {v} _ {A r} \end{array}
$$

这里已考虑了 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega'}{\mathrm{d}t} = 0$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} _ {A e} & = \frac {\mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {\Omega}}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \overrightarrow {O A} + \boldsymbol {\Omega} \times (\boldsymbol {\Omega} \times \overrightarrow {O A}) = \boldsymbol {\Omega} \cdot \times (\boldsymbol {\Omega} \times \overrightarrow {O A}) \\ & \quad \boldsymbol {a} _ {A c} = 2 \boldsymbol {\Omega} \times \boldsymbol {v} _ {A r} = 2 \boldsymbol {\Omega} \times (\boldsymbol {\omega} ^ {\prime} \times \overrightarrow {O A}) \end{array}
$$

可用图中的 Oxyz 坐标系表达，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Omega & = \Omega k, \quad \omega^ {\prime} = - \Omega \csc \alpha (\cos \alpha i + \sin \alpha k) \\ & \overrightarrow {O A} = h \sec \alpha (\cos 2 \alpha i + \sin 2 \alpha k) \end{array}
$$

具体计算略.

方法四: 直接求导的方法, 但不能由上述的 $\overrightarrow{OA} = h\sec \alpha (\cos 2\alpha i + \sin 2\alpha k)$ , 由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\overrightarrow{OA}}{\mathrm{d}t} = v_A$ , 原因是这个 $\overrightarrow{OA}$ 式子只在 $A$ 处于最高点时才成立, 而被求导的式子必须在任何时刻都成立. 同样也不能从已算得的 $v_A = 2\Omega h\cos \alpha j$ 对 $t$ 求导得 $a_A$ , 因为 $v_A$ 的上述式子也只适用于 $A$ 位于最高点时的时刻, 不是任何时刻都成立.
