直接求导需作如下计算：

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} = \frac {\mathrm{d} \overrightarrow {O A}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \boldsymbol {\omega} \times \overrightarrow {O A}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {a} _ {A} & = \frac {\mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {v} _ {A}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \overrightarrow {O A} + \omega \times \frac {\mathrm{d} \overrightarrow {O A}}{\mathrm{d} t} \\ & = (\boldsymbol {\Omega} \times \boldsymbol {\omega}) \times \overrightarrow {O A} + \boldsymbol {\omega} \times (\boldsymbol {\omega} \times \overrightarrow {O A}) \end{array}
$$

以下不再求导,可用图中的动坐标系表达的各个式子.

5.2.5 直杆 A 铰接于 U 形管夹板的 $OO'$ 轴上，该板连在旋转的竖直轴的端点，竖直轴以图 5.23 所示的角速度 $\Omega$ 转动，杆 A 与竖直轴 z 的夹角 $\theta$ 以不变的速率 $\dot{\theta} = -p$ 变化。求杆 A 的角速度 $\omega$ 与角加速度 $\beta$ 。

解 取转动参考系 $O''xyz, y$ 轴与 $OO'$ 保持平行，可见此动参考系的角速度为 $\Omega k$ .

杆 $A$ 的角速度

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega = \omega_ {r} + \omega_ {e}} \\ & {\omega_ {r} = - \dot {\theta} j = p j, \omega_ {e} = \Omega k} \\ & {\quad \therefore \omega = p j + \Omega k} \\ & {\beta = \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (p j + \Omega k)} \\ & {= p \frac {\mathrm{d} j}{\mathrm{d} t} = p \Omega k \times j = - p \Omega i} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/f716df0f5f5d445453b1077ade439579386033c11a4f83076cd278b6f79f0b9d.jpg)



图5.23


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/c31ae42f25b828d7522a265fbb977fd678d923f42b9126e4315c2f9554277965.jpg)



图5.24


5.2.6 飞机重心 G 做匀速直线运动, 坐标系 xyz 的原点置于 G 点, 飞机相对于此坐标系的角速度为: 滚转 $\omega_{x}=0.5s^{-1}$ , 俯仰 $\omega_{y}=0.3s^{-1}$ , 偏航 $\omega_{z}=0.1s^{-1}$ , 现有一旅客在重心 G 之后 30m, 高于 G 点 2m, 以相对于机身的速度 $v_{r}=1m\cdot s^{-1}$ 沿与 x 轴相反的方向走向机尾, 求此旅客的加速度.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega = 0. 5 i + 0. 3 j + 0. 1 k} \\ & {r = - 3 0 i + 2 k} \\ & {v _ {r} = - 1 i, \quad a _ {r} = 0} \\ & {a = a _ {r} + a _ {e} + a _ {c} = a _ {r} + \omega \times (\omega \times r) + \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} \times r + 2 \omega \times v _ {r}} \\ & {= 0 + \omega (\omega \cdot r) - \omega^ {2} r + 0 + 2 \omega \times v _ {r}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = - 1 4. 8 (0. 5 i + 0. 3 j + 0. 1 k) \\ & \quad - [ (0. 5) ^ {2} + (0. 3) ^ {2} + (0. 1) ^ {2} ] (- 3 0 i + 2 k) \\ & \quad + (0. 5 i + 0. 3 j + 0. 1 k) \times (- 1 i) \\ & = 3. 1 i - 4. 6 4 j - 1. 5 8 k (\mathrm {m/ s ^ {2}}) \end{array}
$$

5.2.7 圆盘以匀角速 $\Omega$ 绕其中心轴 $z$ 转动，架子 $A$ 以匀角速度 $\omega_{2}$ 绕其轴线 $y'$ ( $y' \parallel y$ ) 转动。同时，整个装置以角速度 $\omega_{1}$ 绕 $X$ 轴转动。求架子 $A$ 使圆盘处于图5.25所示位置时圆盘的角速度 $\omega$ 、角加速度 $\beta$ 以及圆盘上最高点 $P$ 的速度和加速度，图中 $xyz$ 坐标轴固连于架子 $A$ 。在此时， $x$ 轴与固定坐标 $X$ 轴平行。设圆盘半径为 $r, y, y'$ 轴间距为 $b, x, X$ 轴间距为 $c, z$ 与 $Z$ 轴间的水平间距可以忽略。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/2d2bfa3410290f22490cfeb111ebc601a670fc0cc867a70d36db73263f863276.jpg)



图5.25


解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega = \omega_ {1} i + \omega_ {2} j + \Omega k} \\ & {\beta = \omega_ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} j}{\mathrm{d} t} + \Omega \frac {\mathrm{d} k}{\mathrm{d} t} = \omega_ {2} \omega_ {1} i \times j + \Omega (\omega_ {1} i + \omega_ {2} j) \times k} \\ & {\quad = \omega_ {2} \Omega i - \omega_ {1} \Omega j + \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} k} \\ & {r _ {P} = r _ {1} + r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime} = - c k + b i + r i} \\ & {v _ {P} = \frac {\mathrm{d} r _ {P}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d} r _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} t} + \frac {\mathrm{d} r _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} + \frac {\mathrm{d} r ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} t}} \\ & {\quad = \omega_ {1} \times r _ {1} + (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2}) \times r _ {2} + (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2} + \Omega) \times r ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\quad = \omega_ {1} \times (r _ {1} + r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime}) + \omega_ {2} \times (r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime}) + \Omega \times r ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\quad = \omega_ {1} i \times (- c k + b i + r i) + \omega_ {2} j \times (b + r) i + \Omega k \times r i} \\ & {\quad = (c \omega_ {1} + r \Omega) j - (b + r) \omega_ {2} k} \\ & {a _ {P} = \frac {\mathrm{d} v _ {P}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} [ \omega_ {1} \times (r _ {1} + r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime}) + \omega_ {2} \times (r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime}) + \Omega \times r ^ {\prime} ]} \\ & {\quad = \omega_ {1} \times \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (r _ {1} + r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime}) + \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega_ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} \times (r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime}) + \omega_ {2} \times \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime})} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {+ \frac {\mathrm{d} \Omega}{\mathrm{d} t} \times r ^ {\prime} + \Omega \times \frac {\mathrm{d} r ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} t}} \\ & {= \omega_ {1} \times v _ {P} + (\omega_ {1} \times \omega_ {2}) \times (r _ {2} + r ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\quad + \omega_ {2} \times [ (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2}) \times r _ {2} + (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2} + \Omega) \times r ^ {\prime} ]} \\ & {\quad + [ (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2}) \times \Omega ] \times r ^ {\prime} + \Omega \times [ (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2} + \Omega) \times r ^ {\prime} ]} \\ & {= \omega_ {1} i \times [ (c \omega_ {1} + r \Omega) j - (b + r) \omega_ {2} k ] + (\omega_ {1} i \times \omega_ {2} j) \times (b + r) i} \\ & {\quad + \omega_ {2} j \times [ (\omega_ {1} i + \omega_ {2} j) \times b i + (\omega_ {1} i + \omega_ {2} j + \Omega k) \times r i ]} \\ & {\quad + [ (\omega_ {1} i + \omega_ {2} j) \times \Omega k ] \times r i + \Omega k \times [ (\omega_ {1} i + \omega_ {2} j + \Omega k) \times r i ]} \\ & {= \omega_ {1} (c \omega_ {1} + r \Omega) k + (b + r) \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} j + (b + r) \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} j - b \omega_ {2} ^ {2} i} \\ & {\quad - r \omega_ {2} ^ {2} i + r \omega_ {1} \Omega k - r \Omega^ {2} i} \\ & {= - [ r \Omega^ {2} + (b + r) \omega_ {2} ^ {2} ] i + 2 (b + r) \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} j + (c \omega_ {1} ^ {2} + 2 r \omega_ {1} \Omega) k} \end{array}
$$

注意:用这种求导的方法须注意 $\frac{d\omega_{1}}{dt}$ 、 $\frac{d\omega_{2}}{dt}$ 、 $\frac{d\Omega}{dt}$ 的计算不能乱套公式, $\frac{d\omega_{1}}{dt}\neq\omega\times\omega_{1}$ , $\frac{d\omega_{1}}{dt}=0$ .∵ $\omega_{1}=\omega_{1}i$ ,这个i是XYZ坐标系中的i,不随t而变, $\frac{d\omega_{2}}{dt}\neq\omega\times\omega_{2}$ , $\frac{d\omega_{2}}{dt}\neq\omega_{2}\times\omega_{2}$ , $\frac{d\omega_{2}}{dt}=\omega_{1}\times\omega_{2},\because\omega_{2}=\omega_{2}j$ ,这个j是 $y'$ 轴的单位矢量,在此时,它绕X轴以 $\omega_{1}$ 的角速度转动. $\frac{d\Omega}{dt}\neq\Omega\times\Omega,\frac{d\Omega}{dt}=\omega\times\Omega$ 这样写虽然结果是对的,但严格讲不妥, $\because\Omega=\Omega k$ ,这个k是Z轴的单位矢量,此时它以 $\omega_{1}+\omega_{2}$ 的角速度(以 $\omega_{2}$ 的角速度绕 $y'$ 轴转动, $y'$ 轴又以 $\omega_{1}$ 的角速度绕X轴转动)转动.同样, $r_{2}=bi$ 和 $r'=ri$ 两个i的时间导数是不同的. $a_{P}=\frac{dv_{P}}{dt}\neq\frac{d}{dt}\left[(c\omega_{1}+r\Omega)j-(b+r)\omega_{2}k\right]$ .

方法二:考虑圆盘中心 O 点平动参考系作为动系.

先求 O 点的绝对速度和绝对加速度, 取以角速度 $\omega_{1}$ 绕 X 轴转动的动参考系 XYZ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {O} = \pmb {v} _ {o e} + v _ {o r}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {o e} = \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times (\pmb {r} _ {1} + \pmb {r} _ {2}) = \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times (- c \pmb {k} + b \pmb {i}) = c \omega_ {1} \pmb {j}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {o r} = \omega_ {2} \times \pmb {r} _ {2} = \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} \times b \pmb {i} = - b \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {o} = c \omega_ {1} \pmb {j} - b \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {o} = \pmb {a} _ {o e} + \pmb {a} _ {o r} + \pmb {a} _ {o c}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {o e} = \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times [ \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times (\pmb {r} _ {1} + \pmb {r} _ {2}) ]} \\ & {\qquad = \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times [ \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times (- c \pmb {k} + b \pmb {i}) ] = c \omega_ {1} ^ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {o r} = \frac {\mathrm{d}   \omega_ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \pmb {r} _ {2} + \omega_ {2} \times (\omega_ {2} \times \pmb {r} _ {2})} \\ & {\qquad = 0 + \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} \times (\omega_ {2} \pmb {j} \times b \pmb {i}) = - b \omega_ {2} ^ {2} \pmb {i}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {o c} = 2   \omega_ {1} \times \pmb {v} _ {o r} = 2 \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times (- b \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}) = 2 b \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \pmb {j}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {o} = - b \omega_ {2} ^ {2} \pmb {i} + 2 b \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + c \omega_ {1} ^ {2} \pmb {k}} \end{array}
$$

下面计算 $v_{P}$ 和 $a_{P}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {P} = \pmb {v} _ {P e} + \pmb {v} _ {P r} = \pmb {v} _ {o} + \pmb {v} _ {P r}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {P r} = \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r} ^ {\prime} = (\omega_ {1} \pmb {i} + \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + \Omega \pmb {k}) \times r \pmb {i}} \\ & {\qquad = r \Omega \pmb {j} - r \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {P} = (c \omega_ {1} + r \Omega) \pmb {j} -, (b + r) \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P} = \pmb {a} _ {P e} + \pmb {a} _ {P r} = \pmb {a} _ {o} + \pmb {a} _ {P r}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P r} = \frac {\mathrm{d} v _ {P r}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (\pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {\omega}}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \pmb {r} ^ {\prime} + \pmb {\omega} \times \frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {r} ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \beta \times \pmb {r} ^ {\prime} + \pmb {\omega} \times (\pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\qquad = (\omega_ {2} \Omega \pmb {i} - \omega_ {1} \Omega \pmb {j} + \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}) \times r \pmb {i}} \\ & {\qquad + (\omega_ {1} \pmb {i} + \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + \Omega \pmb {k}) \times [ (\omega_ {1} \pmb {i} + \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + \Omega \pmb {k}) \times r \pmb {i} ]} \\ & {\qquad = - (r \Omega^ {2} + r \omega_ {2} ^ {2}) \pmb {i} + 2 r \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + 2 r \omega_ {1} \Omega \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P} = - [ r \Omega^ {2} + (b + r) \omega_ {2} ^ {2} ] \pmb {i} + 2 (b + r) \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + (c \omega_ {1} ^ {2} + 2 r \omega_ {1} \Omega) \pmb {k}} \end{array}
$$

方法三:考虑以角速度 $\omega_{1}$ 绕 X 轴转动的动参考系.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {P e} = \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times (\pmb {r} _ {1} + \pmb {r} _ {2} + \pmb {r} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\qquad = \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times (- c \pmb {k} + b \pmb {i} + r \pmb {i}) = c \omega_ {1} \pmb {j}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {P r} = \pmb {\omega} _ {2} \times \pmb {r} _ {2} + (\pmb {\omega} _ {2} + \pmb {\Omega}) \times \pmb {r} ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\qquad = \omega_ {2} \pmb {j} \times b \pmb {i} + (\omega_ {2} \pmb {j} + \Omega \pmb {k}) \times r \pmb {i} = r \Omega \pmb {j} - (b + r) \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {v} _ {P} = \pmb {v} _ {P e} + \pmb {v} _ {P r} = (c \omega_ {1} + r \Omega) \pmb {j} - (b + r) \omega_ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P e} = \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times [ \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times (- c \pmb {k} + b \pmb {i} + r \pmb {i}) ] = c \omega_ {1} ^ {2} \pmb {k}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P r} = \ddot {\omega} _ {2} \times (\omega_ {2} \times r _ {2}) + (\omega_ {2} + \Omega) \times [ (\omega_ {2} + \Omega) \times r ^ {\prime} ]} \\ & {\qquad = - (b \omega_ {2} ^ {2} - r \omega_ {2} ^ {2} - r \Omega^ {2}) \pmb {i}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P c} = 2   \omega_ {1} \times \pmb {v} _ {P r} = 2 \omega_ {1} \pmb {i} \times [ r \Omega j - (b + r) \omega_ {2} k ]} \\ & {\qquad = 2 r \omega_ {1} \Omega k + 2 (b + r) \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} j} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {P} = \pmb {a} _ {P e} + \pmb {a} _ {P r} + \pmb {a} _ {P c}} \\ & {\qquad = - [ r \Omega^ {2} + (b + r) \omega_ {2} ^ {2} ] i + 2 (b + r) \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} j + (c \omega_ {1} ^ {2} + 2 r \omega_ {1} \Omega) k} \end{array}
$$

方法四:考虑随 O 点又平动又转动的参考系为动系,此动系的转动角速度为 $\omega_{1} + \omega_{2}$ .

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {P e} = \boldsymbol {v} _ {o} + (\omega_ {1} + \omega_ {2}) \times \boldsymbol {r} ^ {\prime}
$$

用方法二已算出的 $v_{o}=c\omega_{1}j-b\omega_{2}k$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} \pmb {v} _ {P e} = c \omega_ {1} \pmb {j} - b \omega_ {2} \pmb {k} + (\omega_ {1} \pmb {i} + w _ {2} \pmb {j}) \times r \pmb {i} = c \omega_ {1} \pmb {j} - (b + r) \omega_ {2} \pmb {k} \\ \pmb {v} _ {P r} = \pmb {\Omega} \times \pmb {r ^ {\prime}} = \pmb {\Omega} \pmb {k} \times r \pmb {i} = r \pmb {\Omega} \pmb {j} \\ \pmb {v} _ {P} = \pmb {v} _ {P e} + \pmb {v} _ {P r} = (c \omega_ {1} + r \Omega) \pmb {j} - (b + r) \omega_ {2} \pmb {k} \end{array}
$$

采用方法二、三、四，在写 $v_{e}$ 、 $v_{r}$ 、 $a_{e}$ 、 $a_{r}$ 时都须十分小心，因此用方法一出错的可能性小些。

5.2.8 如图 5.26 所示, 圆锥滚子在水平的圆锥环形支座上做纯滚动, 滚子底面半径 $R = 10 \sqrt{2} \, cm$ , 顶角 $2\alpha = 90^{\circ}$ , 滚子中心 A 沿其轨迹运动的速率 $v_{A} = 20 \, cm \cdot s^{-1}$ , 求圆锥滚子上点 B 和 C 的速度和加速度的大小.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/c79754d08d91d0a65c784a450efe3cf048c5ded11710c165ec1df4a3e7b1f4ad.jpg)



图5.26


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/ed9c88a2-770b-4428-9bc5-11b5da24c4e0/005741cf3ba356a9e3b34854f34873af1ff7c6b5faf34c67f588813d78ea96dc.jpg)


解 圆锥滚子滚动时, A 点绕 O 在水平面内做圆周运动, 如图 5.27 所示, 角速度 $\omega_{1}$ 为


图5.27


$$
\omega_ {1} = \frac {v _ {A}}{O A} = \frac {v _ {A}}{A B} = \frac {v _ {A}}{R} = \frac {2 0}{1 0 \sqrt {2}} = \sqrt {2} s ^ {- 1}
$$

滚子做纯滚动， $C$ 点速度为零，

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {C} = 0
$$

O 点是固定点, 在图 5.27 所示位置, OC 为瞬时转轴, 另一个角速度分量 $\omega_{2}$ 必在 AO 的延长线上.

$$
\begin{array}{c} {2 \alpha = 9 0 ^ {\circ},} \\ {\omega = \sqrt {2}   \omega_ {1} = 2 \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1}} \end{array}
$$

OB 线与转轴垂直, $OB=\sqrt{2}R=20cm$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & v _ {B} = \omega \cdot O B = 2 \times 2 0 = 4 0 \mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1} \\ & a _ {B} = \omega \times (\omega \times \overrightarrow {O B}) + \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \overrightarrow {O B} \\ & = - \omega^ {2} \overrightarrow {O B} + (\omega_ {1} \times \omega) \times \overrightarrow {O B} \end{array}
$$

第一项沿 BO 方向, 第二项沿 CO 方向, 互相垂直, 故

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & a _ {B} = \sqrt {(- \omega^ {2} O B) ^ {2} + (\omega_ {1} \omega \sin 4 5 ^ {\circ} \cdot O B) ^ {2}} \\ & \quad = O B \sqrt {(- 2 ^ {2}) ^ {2} + \left(\sqrt {2} \times 2 \times \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) ^ {2}} = 4 0 \sqrt {5} (\mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2}) \\ & a _ {C} = \omega \times (\omega \times \overrightarrow {O C}) + \frac {\mathrm{d} \omega}{\mathrm{d} t} \times \overrightarrow {O C} \\ & \quad = (\omega_ {1} \times \omega) \times \overrightarrow {O C} \\ & a _ {C} = \omega_ {1} \omega \sin 4 5 ^ {\circ} \cdot O C = \omega_ {1} \omega \sin 4 5 ^ {\circ} \cdot O B = 4 0 \mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2} \end{array}
$$

方法二:考虑随 OA 转动的动参考系,滚子对动系的角速度为 $\omega_{2}$ , 角加速度 $\frac{\partial \omega_{2}}{dt}=0$ .

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} _ {B} = \pmb {v} _ {B e} + \pmb {v} _ {B r} = \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times \overrightarrow {O B} + \pmb {\omega} _ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O B}} \\ & {\pmb {a} _ {B} = \pmb {a} _ {B e} + \pmb {a} _ {B r} + \pmb {a} _ {B c}} \\ & {\qquad = \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times (\pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times \overrightarrow {O B}) + \pmb {\omega} _ {2} \times (\pmb {\omega} _ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O B}) + 2 \pmb {\omega} _ {1} \times (\pmb {\omega} _ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O B})} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & v _ {B} = \omega_ {1} R + \omega_ {2} R = 4 0 \mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 1} \\ & a _ {B} = \sqrt {(\omega_ {1} ^ {2} R + 2 \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} R) ^ {2} + (\omega_ {2} ^ {2} R) ^ {2}} = 4 0 \sqrt {5} \mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2} \\ & v _ {C} = v _ {C e} + v _ {C r} = \omega_ {1} \times \overrightarrow {O C} + \omega_ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O C} \\ & a _ {C} = a _ {C e} + a _ {C r} + a _ {C c} \\ & \quad = \omega_ {1} \times (\omega_ {1} \times \overrightarrow {O C}) + \omega_ {2} \times (\omega_ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O C}) + 2 \omega_ {1} \times (\omega_ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O C}) \\ & v _ {C} = \omega_ {1} R - \omega_ {2} R = 0 \\ & a _ {C} = \sqrt {(\omega_ {1} ^ {2} R - 2 \omega_ {1} \omega_ {2} R) ^ {2} + (\omega_ {2} ^ {2} R) ^ {2}} = 4 0 \mathrm{cm} \cdot \mathrm{s} ^ {- 2} \end{array}
$$

这里用了方法一得到的 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ 作上述计算，如果一开始就采取方法二，则先按方法一那样算出 $\omega_{1}$ ，再由 $v_{C} = 0$ 得 $\omega_{2}$ ，再用上述方法算 $v_{B},a_{B}$ 和 $a_{C}$ .

再就5.2.6、5.2.7、5.2.8三题中 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t}$ 的计算作一点说明：5.2.6、5.2.8两题的几个转轴都通过固定点，用 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = \frac{\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} +\Omega \times \omega$ 计算时，可采用统一的动坐标系，5.2.6题可用固连于飞机的动坐标系， $\frac{\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = 0,\omega \times \omega = 0$ ，故 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = 0,5.2.8$ 题，取随 $OA$ 转动的动坐标系， $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = 0,\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = \omega_1\times \omega = \omega_1\times (\omega_1 + \omega_2) = \omega_1\times \omega_2$ ，如取固连于滚子的动坐标系， $\frac{\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}\omega_1}{\mathrm{d}t}\neq 0,\frac{\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}\omega_2}{\mathrm{d}t}$ $= 0,\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = \frac{\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}\omega_1}{\mathrm{d}t}.5.2.7$ 题中圆盘的角速度 $\omega = \omega_{1} + \omega_{2} + \Omega ,\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = \frac{\mathrm{d}\omega_{1}}{\mathrm{d}t} +\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega_{2}}{\mathrm{d}t} +\frac{\mathrm{d}\Omega}{\mathrm{d}t},$ 因 $\omega_{1}$ 、 $\omega_{2},\Omega$ 的转轴不共点，计算 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega_1}{\mathrm{d}t},\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega_2}{\mathrm{d}t},\frac{\mathrm{d}\Omega}{\mathrm{d}t}$ 在使用公式 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} = \frac{\widetilde{\mathrm{d}}\omega}{\mathrm{d}t} +\Omega \times \omega$ 时，必须采用不同的动坐标系.算 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega_1}{\mathrm{d}t}$ 可用静坐标系，算 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega_2}{\mathrm{d}t}$ 用XYZ动坐标系，算 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\Omega}{\mathrm{d}t}$ 用固连于架子 $A$ 的xyz动坐标系.

5.2.9 图 5.28 是一个双重差动机构, 曲柄 3 绕固定轴 AB 转动、在曲柄上活动地套一行星齿轮 4, 行星齿轮由半径分别为 $r_{1}=5cm$ 和 $r_{2}=2cm$ 的两个锥齿轮固连而成, 两锥齿轮又分别与半径分别为 $R_{1}=10cm$ 、 $R_{2}=5cm$ 的另两个锥齿轮 1、2 啮合, 齿轮 1、2 均绕 AB 轴转动, 均不与曲柄相连, 它们的角速度分别为 $\omega_{1}=4.5rad/s$ , $\omega_{2}=9rad/s$ , 转动方向相同. 求曲柄 3 的角速度 $\omega_{3}$ 和行星齿轮相对于曲柄的角速度 $\omega_{43}$ .

解 设 $\omega_{1}$ 、 $\omega_{2}$ 均沿 BA 方向，假定 $\omega_{3}$ 也沿 BA 方向（即与 $\omega_{1}$ 、 $\omega_{2}$ 同向），行星齿轮相对于曲柄 3 的角速度 $\omega_{43}$ 向左（图示时刻），即指向 O 的方向，如图 5.29 所示。

由纯滚动，

$$
\begin{array}{l} {(\omega_ {3} + \omega_ {4 3}) \times \overrightarrow {O C} = \omega_ {1} \times \overrightarrow {O _ {1} D}} \\ {(\omega_ {3} + \omega_ {4 3}) \times \overrightarrow {O E} = \omega_ {2} \times \overrightarrow {O _ {2} F}} \end{array}
$$

即

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega_ {3} R _ {1} - \omega_ {4 3} r _ {1} = \omega_ {1} R _ {1} \\ \omega_ {3} R _ {2} + \omega_ {4 3} r _ {2} = \omega_ {2} R _ {2} \end{array}
$$

$$
1 0 \omega_ {3} - 5 \omega_ {4 3} = 4. 5 \times 1 0 = 4 5\tag{1}
$$

$$
5 \omega_ {3} + 2 \omega_ {4 3} = 9 \times 5 = 4 5\tag{2}
$$

图5.28
